### [1] [RealVDeblur: One-Step Diffusion for Generalizable Real-World Video Deblurring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20628)
*Renbiao Jin,Mingxin Yang,Yutian Chen,Junhao Zhuang,Xin Cai,Mulin Yu,Linning Xu,Wenxian Yu,Danping Zou,Shi Guo,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RealVDeblur，一种基于生成式的高效视频去模糊框架。通过3D高斯溅射构建真实训练数据，利用视频扩散先验并结合帧级编码与单步蒸馏技术，解决复杂运动模糊问题，显著提升下游任务（如3D重建）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界视频去模糊面临运动模式多样、退化复杂及真实训练数据稀缺的挑战，而强大的恢复能力对移动成像和3D重建等下游管线至关重要。

Method: 1. 构建基于3D高斯溅射和高帧率视频的大规模物理真实模糊合成流水线；2. 采用视频扩散先验，禁用VAE的时间压缩并引入帧级编码以应对帧间模糊变化；3. 将多步扩散采样蒸馏为高效单步生成器，并使用无训练时间窗口掩码稳定长视频推理。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准测试中展现出卓越的感知质量、语义保真度和时间一致性，并在严重运动模糊下的3D重建下游任务中表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RealVDeblur通过结合逼真的数据合成与优化的扩散模型架构，有效解决了野外环境下的视频去模糊难题，提升了实际应用中的稳定性和效果。

Abstract: Real-world video deblurring remains challenging due to diverse motion patterns, complex degradations, and the scarcity of realistic training data, yet robust restoration is critical for downstream pipelines such as mobile imaging and 3D reconstruction. This work presents \textbf{RealVDeblur}, an efficient generative framework designed to improve in-the-wild robustness under diverse real capture conditions. First, a large-scale, physically grounded blur synthesis pipeline is constructed from scene-level 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) assets and high-frame-rate videos, providing realistic training data covering both camera-induced and object-motion blur. Second, a video diffusion prior is leveraged for restoration; to better accommodate frame-dependent blur variations, temporal compression in the VAE is disabled and a frame-wise encoding scheme is adopted. For practical deployment on long videos, multi-step diffusion sampling is distilled into an efficient one-step generator, and a training-free Temporal Window Mask stabilizes inference beyond the training horizon with constant memory usage. Extensive experiments on diverse real-world benchmarks demonstrate strong perceptual quality, semantic fidelity, and temporal consistency on unseen videos, as well as improved robustness in downstream 3D reconstruction under severe motion blur. Project page: https://rbjin.github.io/RealVDeblur

</details>


### [2] [Masked Topology Modeling for Self-Supervised Learning on Parametric CAD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20642)
*Heinrich Jiang,Jennifer Jang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对B-Rep CAD数据的自监督预训练方法MTM，通过重建面邻接图的边属性来解决数据稀缺问题，并结合对比学习和掩码重建在ABC等数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管CAD设计广泛应用，但缺乏原生可编辑、参数化的边界表示（B-Rep）格式数据集。因此，亟需开发数据高效的方法以应对这一领域的挑战。

Method: 1. 提出掩码拓扑建模（MTM）任务：利用B-Rep特有的面邻接图结构，掩码部分边，并通过编码器后消息传递的面特征预测每条掩码边的凸性和曲线类型。2. 结合MoCo风格的动量队列对比学习，使用B-Rep感知的数据增强。3. 引入基于BFS连接的面区域掩码重建目标。4. 在ABC数据集及新生成的程序化数据集上进行预训练。

Result: 该方法在多个基准测试中表现出强大的性能，证明了其在处理B-Rep CAD数据方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过利用B-Rep的固有拓扑结构进行自监督预训练，可以有效缓解CAD领域数据稀缺的问题，为后续下游任务提供高质量的表征。

Abstract: Computer aided design (CAD) is ubiquitous: virtually any modern object was designed using editable CAD tools. However, with the shortage of available CAD datasets in its native editable and parametric format, boundary representation (B-Rep), it is ever more important to develop data-efficient methods for this domain.
  We present a new self-supervised pretraining task, Masked Topology Modeling (MTM), that leverages the face-adjacency graph, an induced structure unique to B-reps that the encoder can be asked to reconstruct. MTM masks a fraction of edges and trains a small head to predict each masked edge's convexity and curve type from the encoder's post-message-passing face features.
  We combine MTM with a MoCo-style momentum-queue contrastive learning over B-rep-aware augmentations, a BFS-connected face-region masked-reconstruction objective, and pretraining on the ABC dataset and our new procedurally generated dataset to show strong performance on a number of benchmarks.

</details>


### [3] [Axolotl3D: a Unified Framework for Faithful 3D Shape Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20660)
*Anita Hu,Maria Shugrina*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Axolotl3D是一个多模态、感知遮挡的3D生成模型，通过联合图像、可见性掩码、相机参数和部分点云，解决了现有模型在遮挡和多视角场景下的局限性，实现了可控的3D补全与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D生成模型依赖完整可视性和单视图输入，限制了其在多视角、遮挡或编辑场景中的应用；现有工作虽分别解决这些问题，但缺乏统一框架来处理多样化条件信号下的可控3D补全。

Method: 提出Axolotl3D模型，联合条件输入包括图像、可见性掩码、相机参数和部分点云。其中点云作为几何锚点促进忠实形状补全，相机参数确保共享3D坐标系下的一致性多视角对齐。采用统一训练策略，从大规模3D数据合成多样化的条件组合，实现强大的跨模态推理能力。

Result: 在Toys4K和OmniObject3D数据集上的实验表明，该模型在清洁和遮挡设置下均达到最先进性能，并在真实世界重建和几何一致性的编辑任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: Axolotl3D通过整合多种条件信号和统一训练策略，有效解决了遮挡和多视角条件下的3D生成难题，为3D内容创建提供了更灵活和鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent 3D generative models produce high-quality geometry from a single image using large-scale priors and diffusion architectures. However, they assume complete visibility and single-view inputs, limiting applicability in multi-view, occluded, or editing scenarios. Although prior works address these challenges individually, they lack a unified framework for controllable 3D completion under diverse conditioning signals. We present Axolotl3D, a multi-modal and occlusion-aware 3D generation model that jointly conditions on images, visibility masks, camera parameters, and a partial point cloud. The point cloud serves as a geometric anchor promoting faithful shape completion, while camera parameters ensure consistent multi-view alignment in a shared 3D coordinate system. A unified training strategy synthesizes diverse conditioning regimes from large-scale 3D data, enabling robust cross-modal reasoning. Experiments on Toys4K and OmniObject3D demonstrate state-of-the-art performance under both clean and occluded settings, as well as strong results in real-world reconstruction and geometry-consistent editing.

</details>


### [4] [ODeform: Learning Continuous 4D Motion for Shape Deformation with Neural ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20670)
*Yordanka Velikova,Mahdi Saleh,Liming Kuang,Benjamin Busam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ODeform，一种基于神经常微分方程的方法，用于在3D空间中建模可变形物体的连续4D动态，解决了现有方法离散时间步或计算昂贵的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可变形物体建模方法（如学习法或物理模拟器）要么使用离散时间步，要么计算量过大，无法满足实时应用需求。

Method: 将3D点云和物理条件（如材料属性）映射到统一的潜在空间，通过求解该空间中的常微分方程来建模连续形变流，无需离散时间步。

Result: 在未见的物理参数配置下，运动预测精度优于基线方法；成功迁移至具有新形状的实时3D捕获对象，并实现了有效的动力学插值和外推。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效、高效地建模连续形变，具备优秀的泛化能力和实时性潜力。

Abstract: Modeling continuous object deformation is important for many computer vision and robotics tasks, such as manipulation and simulation. Existing approaches rely on learning-based methods or physics simulators to model shape deformations. However, these approaches either use discrete time steps or are too computationally intensive for real-time applications. We present ODeform, a novel extension of Neural Ordinary Differential Equations to continuous 4D dynamics of deformable objects in 3D space. Our method transforms 3D point clouds and physical conditions (like material properties) into a unified latent space. By solving the resulting ordinary differential equations over time, we model deformations as continuous flows within this learned embedding, eliminating the need for discrete time steps while maintaining computational efficiency. We evaluate our approach on unseen physical parameter configurations, showing improved motion prediction accuracy over baseline methods. Our experiments further demonstrate a successful transfer to real 3D captured objects with novel shapes, along with effective interpolation and extrapolation of the learned dynamics. Our code and data will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [5] [Spatially Grounded Concept Bottleneck Models for Trustworthy Breast Ultrasound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20691)
*Moshiur Rahman Tonmoy,Dunren Che,Haitham Y. Adarbah,Afzel Noore*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据中心的Spatially Grounded Concept Bottleneck Model (SG-CBM)，利用粗略的病变分割作为弱监督信号，解决医学影像中概念瓶颈模型空间不忠实的问题。在乳腺超声数据上，通过定义病灶内区域和后向声学带两个临床相关区域进行训练，显著提升了诊断准确率、概念识别精度及空间对齐度。研究强调了高质量数据监督和系统性可信度验证对于医疗AI部署的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概念瓶颈模型（CBM）虽然具有内在可解释性，但在医学影像应用中，其可信度常受限于监督数据的质量和粒度。具体而言，预测的概念激活往往由无关区域驱动，导致解释缺乏空间忠实性（spatially unfaithful explanations）。

Method: 提出了SG-CBM模型，利用粗粒度的病变轮廓（coarse lesion delineations）作为弱监督信号来鼓励解剖学上合理的概念证据。针对乳腺超声，从每个病变掩膜衍生出两个临床相关的区域：(i) 用于形态学相关概念的病灶内感兴趣区域；(ii) 用于后向现象的后向声学带。使用分组空间接地目标（grouped spatial grounding objective）训练概念图，并通过线性瓶颈分类器保持语义忠实性。

Result: 在五折分层组交叉验证中，SG-CBM提高了诊断AUROC和概念宏观AUROC，并显著增加了概念证据的空间对齐度。此外，通过“训练损坏/测试干净”的标注质量压力测试，量化了监督质量对诊断和空间忠实性的影响。

Conclusion: 结果强调了在设计面向数据质量的监督策略以及为可部署的医疗AI系统进行系统性可信度验证方面的必要性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models provide interpretable-by-design predictions by mediating diagnosis through human-understandable concepts, but in medical imaging, their trustworthiness is often limited by the quality and granularity of available supervision. In particular, predicted concept activations can be driven by irrelevant regions, leading to spatially unfaithful explanations. We study a data-centric spatially grounded Concept Bottleneck Model (SG-CBM) that leverages coarse lesion delineations as weak supervision to encourage anatomically plausible concept evidence. For breast ultrasound, we derive two clinically motivated zones from each lesion mask: (i) an in-lesion region of interest for morphology-related concepts and (ii) a posterior acoustic band for posterior phenomena. We train concept maps using a grouped spatial grounding objective and preserve semantic faithfulness with a linear bottleneck classifier. Across five-fold stratified group cross-validation, the proposed SG-CBM improves diagnostic AUROC and concept macro-AUROC while markedly increasing spatial alignment of concept evidence. We also perform a Train-corrupt/Test-clean annotation-quality stress test to quantify the impact of supervision quality on diagnosis and spatial faithfulness. Overall, the results underscore the need for data-quality-aware supervision design and systematic trustworthiness validation for deployable healthcare AI systems.

</details>


### [6] [DS@GT ARC at ImageCLEFmed GANs 2026: Geometric Filtering for Privacy-Preserving CT Slice Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20692)
*Eric Regina,Richard Arnaud,Samir Hadi Cisneros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结合最优传输条件流匹配与几何后处理过滤的合成肺部CT图像生成框架，在Image-CLEFmed GANs 2026挑战赛中实现了良好的真实性-隐私权衡，但揭示了防止直接图像复制不足以消除深层患者身份信息的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Image-CLEFmed GANs 2026挑战赛开发一种保护隐私的合成肺部CT切片生成框架，旨在平衡生成图像的真实感与患者数据隐私保护。

Method: 1. 核心生成：采用最优传输条件流匹配（Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching）进行隐私导向的训练；2. 后处理过滤：引入“监督者”管道，利用自编码器嵌入、确定性点过程（DPP）和Stein核变薄技术，在学习的几何潜在空间中过滤生成的候选样本。

Result: 最佳模型实现隐私保护得分0.549，FID值为0.3290（具有竞争力的视觉保真度）。几何过滤显著减少了最近邻记忆化和成员推断泄露，但持续的患者重新识别评分表明仍存在深层身份信息风险。

Conclusion: 虽然提出的方法在减少直接图像复制方面有效，但仅防止直接复制不足以移除深层的患者特定解剖学身份特征。这指出了未来隐私保护医学图像生成领域的一个重要研究方向。

Abstract: We present a privacy-preserving framework for synthetic lung CT slice generation developed for the Image-CLEFmed GANs 2026 challenge. The approach combines Optimal Transport Conditional Flow Matching with privacy-oriented training and a post-generation "Supervisor" pipeline that filters generated candidates in learned geometric latent spaces using autoencoder embeddings, Determinantal Point Processes, and Stein Kernel Thinning. Official results show a strong realism-privacy trade-off, with the best-performing model achieving a Privacy Preservation Score of 0.549 and competitive visual fidelity with an FID of 0.3290. While the proposed geometric filtering substantially reduces nearest-neighbor memorization and membership-inference leakage, persistent patient re-identification scores indicate that preventing direct image copying is not sufficient to remove deeper patient-specific anatomical identity, highlighting an important frontier for future privacy-preserving medical image generation.

</details>


### [7] [U-CFR: Uncertainty-Guided Cascade Forward Refinement for Interactive Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20705)
*Elijah Danquah Darko,Min Xian,Terence Soule,Tiankai Yao,Matthew William Anderson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出U-CFR框架，通过边界感知不确定性引导的伪点击实现交互式图像分割的自主自校正，显著减少用户点击次数并提升分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交互式图像分割方法通常需要大量纠正性点击或依赖收敛缓慢的被动细化方案，导致标注效率低下。

Method: 提出Uncertainty-Guided Cascade Forward Refinement (U-CFR)推理时框架。设计双头网络（共享编码器-解码器骨干），包含确保区域一致性的分割头和锐化边界对齐的边缘头。引入边界感知不确定性评分，融合分割不确定性、轮廓梯度和显式边缘预测，以指导内部伪点击的定位，形成级联细化步骤。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上，U-CFR提高了点击效率、初始掩码质量和边界准确性。在Berkeley等挑战性数据集上，所需点击次数减少了超过10%。

Conclusion: U-CFR提供了一种更智能、高效的交互式标注方案，通过自主生成伪点击目标最模糊的边界区域，实现了无需额外手动输入的强校正信号。

Abstract: Interactive image segmentation is critical for efficient image annotation; however, existing methods often require many corrective clicks or rely on passive refinement schemes that converge slowly. We propose Uncertainty-Guided Cascade Forward Refinement (U-CFR), a novel inference-time framework that enables models to autonomously self-correct after each user interaction. U-CFR introduces a boundary-aware uncertainty score that fuses segmentation uncertainty, contour gradients, and explicit edge predictions to guide the placement of internal pseudo-clicks. These self-generated clicks target the most ambiguous boundary regions, providing strong corrective signals without additional manual input. To support this process, we design a dual-head network with a shared encoder-decoder backbone: a segmentation head ensures region consistency, while an edge head sharpens boundary alignment. In inference, U-CFR launches a cascade of refinement steps, where each stage leverages the uncertainty-driven pseudo-clicks to refine the mask progressively. Experiments on standard benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed U-CFR improves click efficiency, initial mask quality, and boundary accuracy. It reduces the required clicks by over 10% on challenging datasets like Berkeley and offers a more intelligent and efficient interactive annotation.

</details>


### [8] [Rethinking Open-World Video Anomaly Detection: Diagnosing Definition Blindness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20780)
*Inpyo Song,Jangwon Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文指出当前开放世界视频异常检测（OWVAD）评估存在“定义盲视”问题，即模型对特定异常定义的响应能力弱。为此提出新的条件评估指标和DeCoS评分规则，显著提升了模型对异常定义的遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWVAD评估未能有效隔离模型对用户指定异常定义的敏感性，导致模型在保持“目标-正常”分离能力的同时，对查询定义几乎无反应，这种现象被称为“定义盲视”。

Method: 1. 分解动态定义评估，揭示现有基准中“目标vs正常”检测权重过高；2. 提出三个定义条件评估指标（DC-Disc, DC-DetΔ, DC-SelΔ）以消除捷径偏差；3. 引入DeCoS（定义对比评分规则），通过减去跨定义的共享异常证据来改进评分。

Result: 在UCF-Crime、XD-Violence和MSAD数据集上实验发现，主流基线模型虽能定位异常时刻但定义跟随能力极弱。应用DeCoS后，最强基线在DC-Disc和DC-DetΔ指标上分别提升7.3-16.0和15.5-28.3 AUROC点。

Conclusion: OWVAD应被评估为“定义条件化的异常评分”，而非简单的“不同提示标签下的异常检测”，强调模型必须真正响应用户定义的异常标准。

Abstract: Open-world video anomaly detection (OWVAD) is expected to detect events that match a user-specified definition of abnormality. This requirement is stronger than generic anomaly localization: in the same video, changing the definition should change which temporal regions are scored as anomalous. We show that current OWVAD evaluation largely fails to isolate this conditional behavior. Standard VAD metrics and the dynamic-definition protocol can be dominated by target-versus-normal separation, allowing models to obtain strong scores while remaining nearly insensitive to the queried definition. We call this failure mode definition blindness. To explain why it is missed, we decompose dynamic-definition evaluation into target-versus-normal detection and target-versus-other-anomaly discrimination, and find that the former receives 7.2-26.8$\times$ more weight across common VAD benchmarks. Motivated by this diagnosis, we introduce three definition-conditioned evaluation metrics, DC-Disc, DC-Det$Δ$, and DC-Sel$Δ$, which progressively remove normal-frame, generic-anomaly, and multi-event selection shortcuts. Experiments on UCF-Crime, XD-Violence, and MSAD reveal that several strong VAD, OWVAD, and general vision language model baselines localize anomalous moments but exhibit weak definition following, often with near-zero definition-response margins. To validate that the failure is actionable, we further introduce DeCoS, a definition-contrastive scoring rule that subtracts anomaly evidence shared across definitions. DeCoS improves the strongest baseline by 7.3-16.0 AUROC points on DC-Disc and 15.5-28.3 points on DC-Det$Δ$. Overall, our results argue that OWVAD should be evaluated as definition-conditioned anomaly scoring, not as anomaly detection under different prompt labels.

</details>


### [9] [3D-GIMP: When 3D Gaussian Inpainting Meets PatchMatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20789)
*Xuening Tian,Dieter Schmalstieg,Shohei Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出3D-GIMP，一种结合生成式修复与PatchMatch的混合范式，用于3D高斯溅射中的高保真物体移除，解决了扩散模型计算昂贵、细节模糊及多视图不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于迭代扩散模型的3D场景编辑方法存在计算成本高、难以生成清晰细节以及因“幻觉漂移”导致多视图不一致和结构伪影的问题。

Method: 3D-GIMP仅在关键参考视图上执行单次生成式修复作为外观先验，随后引入3D感知PatchMatch算法通过对应匹配将纹理传播至其余所有视图，从而避免逐帧扩散的随机性。

Result: 实验表明，3D-GIMP在保持高频细节和数学一致的3D重建方面表现优异，其修复质量与以往多视图扩散方法相当，且在渲染速度和视图一致性上显著优于这些方法。

Conclusion: 3D-GIMP通过优先保证重建一致性而非迭代生成，成功实现了高效、高保真且视图一致性的3D物体移除。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D scene editing have leveraged iterative diffusion models to update input views. However, this process is computationally expensive and struggles to produce sharp details. Meanwhile, ``hallucination drift'' frequently introduces multi-view inconsistencies, leading to structural artifacts when rendering novel viewpoints. To address this problem, we present 3D-GIMP (3D Gaussian Inpainting Meets Patch Matching), a novel hybrid paradigm designed for high-fidelity object removal in 3D Gaussian Splatting. Instead of diffusing every view, 3D-GIMP performs a single generative inpainting on a key reference view, which serves as an appearance prior. We then introduce a 3D-aware PatchMatch algorithm to propagate these reference textures across all remaining views via correspondence matching, effectively bypassing the stochastic nature of frame-by-frame diffusion. By prioritizing reconstructive consistency over iterative generation, 3D-GIMP maintains high-frequency details across arbitrary resolutions while ensuring a mathematically consistent 3D reconstruction. Our experiments demonstrate that 3D-GIMP not only achieves competitive inpainting quality as previous methods using diffusion in multiple views, but also outperforms these methods in rendering speed and view consistency.

</details>


### [10] [Ocular Verification for Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20790)
*Husanpreet Singh,Robert Tran,Ayushree Kharel,Sudipta Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究针对VR头显中非约束采集下的虹膜识别难题，评估了ISO质量指标的局限性，利用生成模型解决数据特异性挑战，并证明结合眼周区域的 multimodal 融合可显著降低错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虹膜识别协议在VR等非约束采集场景下表现不足，需解决离轴注视、光照不均及镜面反射等特定数据挑战，以提升VR环境中的用户认证安全性与准确性。

Method: 1. 在VRBiom数据集上评估ISO/IEC 29794-6虹膜质量指标；2. 使用生成模型处理离轴、光照不均和镜面反射等数据特异性问题；3. 进行单模态（虹膜、眼周）识别及多模态（虹膜+眼周）分数级融合实验。

Result: 部分质量指标（如边缘充分性）在VR数据上失效；图像调整主要提升眼周识别性能；多模态融合使等错误率（EER）比单模态虹膜识别降低了约11%。

Conclusion: 在VR生物特征认证中，单一虹膜识别存在局限，采用多模态融合策略能有效提升识别鲁棒性和准确性，且研究代码将开源以促进可重复性。

Abstract: Virtual reality (VR) headsets (e.g., Meta Quest, Apple Vision Pro) provide a seamless user experience due to their fast, frictionless interaction with the physical world in a simulated environment. User authentication relies on biometric cues such as iris in such headsets. However, traditional iris recognition protocols may not be adequate in cases of unconstrained acquisition, which is typical of VR-based data. In this work, we examine three crucial aspects: (1) evaluating ISO/IEC 29794-6 iris quality metrics on VRBiom dataset and analyzing their limitations, (2) addressing data-specific challenges such as off-axis gaze, non-uniform illumination, and specular reflection using generative models, and (3) performing unimodal (iris, periocular) recognition and multimodal score-level fusion (iris + periocular). We observe that some metrics (e.g., margin adequacy) fail on VR-acquired data; whereas, image adjustments primarily benefit periocular recognition, and multimodal fusion lowers EER by ~11% over unimodal iris recognition performance. We will release the evaluation scripts upon acceptance for reproducibility.

</details>


### [11] [SubSplat: High-Resolution Pixel-aligned 3DGS via Sub-pixel Gaussian Reparameterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20813)
*Jiun Lee,Jaekwang Kim,Sangmin Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SubSplat框架，通过子像素高斯重参数化器(SPGR)和低分辨率特征聚合，解决高分辨率渲染中计算成本与细节质量的权衡问题，实现高效高保真新视角合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有像素对齐的高斯泼溅技术在高分辨率渲染面临两难：提高输入分辨率虽能增加细节，但会导致网络计算成本呈二次方增长；而保持低分辨率输入虽能稳定成本，却导致高斯密度不足并产生伪影。

Method: 1. 引入子像素高斯重参数化器(SPGR)，将主高斯细分细粒度基元，直接从低分辨率特征恢复结构密度；2. 通过特征聚合增强重参数化质量，有效捕捉多视图中的高频细节。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和ACID数据集上的实验表明，SubSplat实现了高保真渲染且具有优越的效率，成功解决了重参数化保真度与网络计算成本之间的权衡问题。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效平衡了像素对齐高斯泼溅中的效率与质量矛盾，验证了从低分辨率特征恢复高密度结构的可行性。

Abstract: Pixel-aligned Gaussian splatting enables efficient and generalizable novel-view synthesis. However, high-resolution rendering faces a critical trade-off where increasing input resolution improves detail at the expense of quadratically rising network computational cost. Conversely, maintaining low-resolution inputs stabilizes this cost but results in insufficient Gaussian density and artifacts. To address this, we propose SubSplat, which introduces Sub-pixel Gaussian Reparameterizer(SPGR) to subdivide primary Gaussians into fine-grained primitives, restoring structural density directly from low-resolution features. We further enhance the reparameterization quality through feature aggregation, which effectively captures high-frequency details across multiple views. Experiments on RealEstate10K and ACID demonstrate that SubSplat achieves high-fidelity rendering with superior efficiency. Our results validate that the proposed framework successfully resolves the trade-off between reparameterization fidelity and network computational cost inherent in pixel-aligned Gaussian Splatting.

</details>


### [12] [Explainable graph attention network for stress recognition (StressGAT) via differential action units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20819)
*Thomas Kassiotis,Stefanos Gkikas,Nikolaos Smyrnis,Giorgos Giannakakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出StressGAT模型，利用图注意力网络结合差分动作单元（Differential Action Units）和多重实例学习（MIL），实现高精度且可解释的个性化压力检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RNN和CNN在处理面部表情动态变化时存在序列瓶颈和刚性网格限制，难以捕捉非线性时间进程；同时，现有深度学习模型缺乏临床部署所需的可解释性，且往往忽视个体基线差异。

Method: 构建StressGAT框架：1. 采用图注意力网络（Graph Attention Network）建模面部关系的归纳偏置以捕捉复杂动态；2. 使用差分动作单元将个体反应相对于中性基线进行归一化，实现个性化识别；3. 集成多重实例学习（MIL）注意力机制以识别峰值压力区间并揭示表达表型。

Result: 在包含58名参与者的多样化压力诱导队列中，采用被试独立（LOSO）交叉验证协议，模型准确率达到88.62%。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持高预测精度的同时提供了可解释性，为个性化情感监测提供了一种稳健、可解释的解决方案，具备临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Stress is a dynamic process characterized by significant individual variability in facial expression. Traditional architectures, such as Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) and Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs), often overlook person-specific baselines or lack the representational capacity to model the non-linear temporal progression of distress due to sequential bottlenecks and rigid grid-based constraints. Furthermore, many deep learning models lack the interpretability required for clinical deployment. This study introduces StressGAT, a Graph Attention Network that leverages the relational inductive bias of graph modeling to capture complex facial dynamics that indicate acute stress. By using Differential Action Units, the framework normalizes individual responses relative to neutral baselines to achieve personalized recognition. The proposed model achieves 88.62\% accuracy on a diverse stress-induction cohort (58 participants) using a subject-independent, Leave-One-Subject-Out (LOSO) cross-validation protocol. Beyond predictive accuracy, the architecture integrates a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) attention mechanism to identify peak stress intervals and reveal distinct expressivity phenotypes. By simultaneously optimizing for accuracy and interpretability, this framework provides a robust, explainable solution for personalized affective monitoring.

</details>


### [13] [Agentic Designer: Progressive Multi-Agent Collaboration for Structure-Aware Interior Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20866)
*Zhijing Yang,Haocheng Lin,Zhihua Xu,Haojie Li,Keze Wang,Liang Lin,Tianshui Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Agentic Designer框架，通过多智能体协作（生成、评估、修正）和渐进共识机制，解决室内布局生成中的结构约束冲突问题，并建立InStruct基准进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型或LLM的单步生成方法缺乏中间几何约束验证机制，导致在复杂房间约束下出现结构碰撞和功能不可行的布局。

Method: 构建Agentic Designer多智能体框架，将布局合成分解为提议、验证和调整三个阶段；协调Generator、Evaluator和Refiner三个专用智能体，通过渐进共识机制在每次放置前执行逐步几何验证和纠正。

Result: 建立了包含18,000+样本的InStruct基准及结构中心指标；定量评估、定性分析和用户研究表明，该方法在严格结构遵循性和功能设计连贯性方面显著优于最先进方法。

Conclusion: Agentic Designer通过迭代式约束验证有效解决了室内布局生成的结构性难题，提升了自动化空间设计的可行性和质量。

Abstract: Generating realistic interior furniture layouts that strictly adhere to architectural constraints (e.g., walls, doors, and windows) remains a fundamental challenge in automated spatial design. Existing approaches, primarily based on one-shot generation using diffusion models or Large Language Models (LLMs), lack explicit mechanisms for intermediate geometric constraint verification, often resulting in structural collisions and functionally infeasible arrangements under complex room constraints. To address these challenges, we propose Agentic Designer, a progressive, multi-agent framework that formulates structure-aware interior layout generation as an iterative and constraint-verified decision process. By decomposing layout synthesis into modular stages of proposal, verification, and adjustment, the framework coordinates three specialized agents, a Generator, an Evaluator, and a Refiner, through a Progressive Consensus Mechanism. This mechanism enforces stepwise geometric validation and correction before each placement is committed, thereby preventing error accumulation. To facilitate this structure-aware paradigm and standardize evaluation, we establish InStruct, a comprehensive benchmark that integrates a dataset comprising over 18,000 high-quality, parametrically annotated samples with a novel suite of structure-centric metrics. Extensive quantitative evaluations, qualitative analyses, and user studies show that Agentic Designer significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, demonstrating substantial improvements in strict structural adherence and functional design coherence.

</details>


### [14] [ViSTR-Bench: Can MLLMs Reason from Continuous Visual Cues in Dynamic Scenes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20868)
*Han Li,Si Liu,Zehao Huang,Dongxin Lyu,Longfei Xu,Jiahui Fu,Daxin Tian,Yuliang Xiu,Naiyan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ViStr-Bench基准，评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在动态场景中的定性空间-时间推理能力，发现当前模型在此类任务上仍存在显著瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs虽在专家级任务中表现优异，但在人类通过持续观察自然发展的基础能力（如空间感知和动态推理）上存在不足；现有基准多关注静态场景或定量预测，缺乏对基于时间线索的直观定性推理的系统评估。

Method: 构建Visual Spatial-Temporal Reasoning Benchmark (ViStr-Bench)，遵循时间强调、推理导向和定性评估原则，涵盖运动感知、空间关系、结果预测和物理动力学四个维度，包含15个子任务和1,340个高质量视频问答对。

Result: 对多种先进专有、开源及专用空间MLLMs进行评估，结果显示尽管这些模型具备强大的通用视频理解能力，但在复杂的空间-时间推理方面仍面临重大挑战，性能远低于人类水平。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs在动态场景中的定性空间-时间推理能力仍有巨大提升空间，ViStr-Bench为系统性评估和改进该领域提供了重要工具。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success across diverse expert-level tasks, but they still struggle with fundamental abilities that humans naturally develop through continuous observation of the real world, such as spatial perception and dynamic reasoning. Recent studies have recognized this gap and introduced dedicated benchmarks to evaluate the spatial-temporal capabilities of MLLMs. However, existing benchmarks mostly focus on static scenes or require exact quantitative predictions, leaving intuitive reasoning from temporal cues largely underexplored. In this paper, we introduce the Visual Spatial-Temporal Reasoning Benchmark (ViSTR-Bench), a novel evaluation suite designed to systematically assess whether MLLMs can perform qualitative reasoning from continuous visual cues in dynamic scenes. Guided by the principles of temporal emphasis, reasoning orientation, and qualitative evaluation, ViSTR-Bench establishes a comprehensive four-dimensional evaluations covering Motion Perception, Spatial Relations, Outcome Prediction, and Physical Dynamics. The benchmark comprises 15 distinct subtasks and 1,340 high-quality video question-answer pairs spanning diverse tabletop, indoor, and outdoor scenarios. Extensive evaluations of a broad spectrum of state-of-the-art proprietary, open-source, and specialized spatial MLLMs reveal that, despite their strong general video understanding capabilities, current models still face substantial bottlenecks in complex spatial-temporal reasoning and remain far below human performance.

</details>


### [15] [Webly Supervised Multi-Label Recognition: Evaluation Benchmark and Dual-Branch Multi-Label Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20874)
*Zhihua Xu,Zhijing Yang,Yufeng Yang,Tianshui Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对网络监督多标签识别（WS-MLR）缺乏统一基准的问题，构建了Web-COCO和Web-Pascal两个数据集，并提出了DBMLCL框架，通过双分支对比学习校正噪声标签，显著提升了识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管单标签的网络监督学习已广泛研究，但多标签版本因缺乏统一基准和公平比较协议而研究不足。旨在填补这一空白，提供标准化的评估环境和更有效的解决方案。

Method: 1. 构建WS-MLR基准：创建Web-COCO（80类）和Web-Pascal（20类）数据集，各含约30万张从互联网检索的图像；统一基线模型设置。
2. 提出DBMLCL框架：采用双分支多标签对比学习，同时学习类别特定的实例级和类别级表示及其相似度，以识别和纠正噪声标签。

Result: 在构建的基准上进行的大量实验表明，DBMLCL框架在性能上优于现有的代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: 提出的基准和数据集促进了该领域的标准化研究，而DBMLCL框架通过对比学习有效解决了噪声标签问题，证明了其在网络监督多标签识别中的优越性。

Abstract: Training deep learning models with freely available web images can reduce their dependence on costly manual annotations. Although webly supervised learning has been widely studied for single-label recognition, its multi-label counterpart remains underexplored, partly due to the lack of unified benchmarks and fair comparison protocols. To address this gap, we construct a benchmark for webly supervised multi-label recognition (WS-MLR), including Web-COCO and Web-Pascal, and re-implement representative baselines under a unified setting. The two datasets cover the same 80 and 20 categories as MS-COCO and Pascal VOC, respectively, and contain about 300 thousand images retrieved from the Internet using category-word combinations as search keywords. We further propose a Dual-Branch Multi-Label Contrastive Learning (DBMLCL) framework, which learns category-specific instance-level and category-level representations together with their similarities to identify and correct noisy labels. Extensive experiments on the benchmark demonstrate that DBMLCL achieves superior performance compared to representative baselines.

</details>


### [16] [WhereEdit: Mask-aware Local Latent Editing for One-Step Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20883)
*Ming Hu,Mingyu Dou,Jianfu Yin,Miaomiao Zhang,Cong Hu,Yao Wang,Bingliang Hu,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WhereEdit框架，通过自动识别语义相关区域并进行自适应局部调制，解决现有单步图像编辑方法缺乏空间控制和语义修改不稳定的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单步文本到图像（T2I）编辑方法主要依赖文本条件进行语义转换，缺乏对“在哪里”编辑的显式空间控制；即使引入空间约束，也难以在目标区域内实现强烈且稳定的语义修改。现有方法执行全局语义传输，限制了单步设置下高强度局部编辑的效果。

Method: 提出WhereEdit框架，将单步编辑重构为局部自适应编辑。该方法从内部模型特征中自动发现可编辑区域，并对目标区域应用自适应局部调制，以增强目标区域的编辑效果，同时保留非目标区域和结构一致性。

Result: 在PIE-Bench基准测试上，WhereEdit一致优于现有的单步图像编辑方法，实现了更高的编辑质量，同时保持了一步生成的效率。带有区域级监督的额外实验进一步强调了显式空间推理对于高质量单步图像编辑的重要性。

Conclusion: 重新审视从空间控制角度进行的单步图像编辑，揭示了发现可编辑区域和实现有效局部语义转换是关键挑战。通过局部自适应编辑策略，WhereEdit解决了全局语义传输的限制，提升了单步图像编辑的质量和稳定性。

Abstract: Recent one-step text-to-image (T2I) models enable efficient image synthesis and provide new opportunities for real-time image editing. However, existing one-step editing methods primarily rely on text conditioning for semantic transformation, lacking explicit spatial control over \textit{where} to edit. More importantly, even when spatial constraints are introduced, these methods often struggle to achieve strong and stable semantic modifications within the target regions. In this work, we revisit one-step image editing from a spatially controlled perspective and identify two key challenges: discovering editable regions and achieving effective localized semantic transformation. We reveal that existing methods perform global semantic transport, which limits high-intensity local editing under the one-step setting. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{WhereEdit}, a framework that reformulates one-step editing as localized adaptive editing. WhereEdit automatically identifies semantically relevant regions from internal model features and applies adaptive local modulation to enhance target-region editing while preserving non-target areas and structural consistency. Experiments on the PIE-Bench benchmark demonstrate that WhereEdit consistently outperforms existing one-step image editing methods, achieving superior editing quality while maintaining the efficiency of one-step generation. Additional experiments with region-level supervision further highlight the importance of explicit spatial reasoning for high-quality one-step image editing.

</details>


### [17] [Engine-Native Editable 3D World Reconstruction with Objects and Lighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20889)
*Junhao Chen,Xinghao Chen,Henghaofan Zhang,Zihao Qiao,Saining Zhang,Yongzhi Li,Ruqi Huang,Sisi Li,Yimin Sheng,Jianyi Zhu,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lumera是一个面向单张图像的引擎原生、光照感知3D场景解析基准和参考流水线。它利用UE5项目构建的大规模数据集，通过适配视觉语言模型（VLM）实现物体框检测和参数化灯光解析，旨在解决现有方法在可编辑性和引擎兼容性上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单图像3D生成方法主要局限于房间级几何结构、烘焙/全局光照或文本驱动生成，缺乏可直接导入标准游戏引擎、支持对象实例检查和移动的“引擎原生”且“光照感知”的可编辑3D场景创建能力。

Method: 1. 构建Lumera-2K数据集：基于2,513个UE5项目，包含373万个组件、6300万个对象实例、10.26万个引擎原生参数化灯光和9.51万个相机视图。
2. Lumera-Box：适配VLM解析对象边界框。
3. Lumera-Light：适配VLM解析参数化灯光元组（位置、颜色、强度）。
4. 组装流程：结合每对象网格重建、HDR环境估计和有界智能体精炼循环，将检测结果组装为完整场景。

Result: 1. 物体检测（Lumera-Box）：在Sanitized Box基准测试中，整体检测、几何、语义和布局得分最强（合并mAP 0.1141, IoU-B 0.2472, F-score 0.2762），优于DetAny3D、SpatialLM等，但在锚点召回率上略逊于WildDet3D。
2. 灯光恢复（Lumera-Light）：几乎恢复所有非空场景（召回率0.998），但单个灯光定位精度有限（F1 0.209@0.5m）；匹配灯光的中位位置误差0.261m，颜色误差ΔE2000为4.59，强度相关系数r=0.628。

Conclusion: 研究确立了参数化灯光作为可测量可编辑场景目标的价值，同时揭示了当前技术在关系结构、灯光召回/强度以及跨引擎泛化方面仍存在瓶颈。

Abstract: Editable 3D scene creation requires object instances and lights that can be inspected, moved, and imported into standard engines, yet existing single-image methods largely stop at room-scale geometry, baked/global illumination, or text-driven generation. We introduce Lumera (Light-aware Unified Engine-native Reconstruction and Assembly), a benchmark and reference pipeline for engine-native, light-aware 3D scene parsing from a single image. Lumera-2K is built from 2,513 UE5 projects and provides 3.73M components, 63M object instances, 102.6K engine-native parametric lights, and 95.1K camera views. On this data, Lumera-Box and Lumera-Light adapt VLM to parse object boxes and parametric light tuples (x,y,z,r,g,b,I), which are assembled with per-object mesh reconstruction, HDR environment estimation, and a bounded agentic refinement loop. In a sanitized box benchmark against DetAny3D, SpatialLM, N3D-VLM, and WildDet3D, Lumera-Box obtains the strongest overall detection, geometry, semantic, and layout scores (merged mAP 0.1141, IoU-B 0.2472, F-score 0.2762), while WildDet3D remains stronger on anchor recall. For lights, Lumera-Light recovers almost all non-empty scenes (recall 0.998) but remains limited at individual-light localization (F1 0.209 at 0.5 m); matched lights have median position error 0.261 m, median ΔE2000 4.59, and intensity Pearson r=0.628. These results establish parametric lights as a measurable editable-scene target and expose remaining bottlenecks in relation structure, light recall/intensity, and cross-engine generalization.

</details>


### [18] [MAGE-Vein: Multi-Instance Age and Gender Estimation from Finger Vein Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20897)
*Katsuki Tanaka,Koichi Ito,Takafumi Aoki,Masakazu Fujio,Yosuke Kaga,Kanade Oshima,Kenta Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAGE-Vein框架，通过多实例、多任务学习解决指静脉年龄估计中的性别偏差和生理混淆问题，在平衡数据集上取得高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指静脉年龄估计因公共数据集存在严重的人口统计学偏差及性别等生理混淆因素而被认为不切实际。

Method: 构建MAGE-Vein框架：1) 采用三指混合特征级融合提取鲁棒的结构性老化特征以抑制噪声；2) 联合优化性别分类任务以消除性别特异性血管变化影响。

Result: 在402名受试者的平衡数据集上，平均绝对误差(MAE)为6.12年，相关系数为0.880。

Conclusion: 研究结果推翻了关于指静脉模态局限性的传统共识，证明先前的估计失败主要源于有偏见的公共数据集，而非模态本身无效。

Abstract: Age estimation from finger vein images has been widely considered impractical due to severe demographic biases in public datasets and physiological confounding factors like gender. To overcome these limitations, we propose MAGE-Vein, a novel multi-instance, multi-task learning framework. Our approach extracts robust structural aging signs by employing a hybrid feature-level fusion of three fingers, effectively suppressing local imaging noise. Furthermore, simultaneous optimization of gender classification conditions the network to effectively eliminate gender-specific vascular variations. Evaluated on a demographically balanced dataset of 402 subjects, MAGE-Vein achieves a mean absolute error of 6.12 years and a correlation of 0.880. Our results not only overturn the conventional consensus regarding the limitations of the finger vein modality but also demonstrate that previous estimation failures were primarily artifacts of biased public datasets. Our code is available at https://github.com/gsisaoki/MAGE-Vein.

</details>


### [19] [DINO-VPT: Hierarchical Visual Prompt Tuning for Joint Physical-Digital Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20900)
*Pierre Gallin-Martel,Mika Feng,Koichi Ito,Takafumi Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DINO-VPT，一种仅依赖视觉的轻量级统一人脸反欺诈框架，通过分层视觉提示调优和动态提示路由网络，在无需多模态融合的情况下实现了优于现有VLM方法的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对欺骗攻击日益多样化带来的需求，现有的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）虽然泛化能力强，但严重依赖复杂的多模态融合和外部文本编码器，导致架构臃肿。

Method: 提出DINO-VPT框架，利用分层视觉提示调优技术；设计提示路由网络（PRN），根据输入特征动态注入提示，从而有效解耦多样的欺骗伪影，避免使用多模态融合。

Result: 在UniAttackData基准测试中，DINO-VPT的准确率高于目前最先进（SOTA）的基于VLM的方法，证明了纯视觉架构在统一FAS任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构合理的纯视觉架构可以在无需多模态监督的情况下，在统一人脸反欺诈任务中达到最先进的性能水平。

Abstract: With the increasing diversity of spoofing attacks, there is a growing demand for unified Face Anti-Spoofing (FAS) models capable of detecting both physical and digital threats. While existing Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate high generalization in this context, they heavily rely on complex multimodal fusion and external text encoders. In this paper, we propose DINO-VPT, a lightweight, vision-only framework leveraging hierarchical visual prompt tuning. By dynamically injecting prompts conditioned on input features via a Prompt Routing Network (PRN), our method effectively disentangles diverse spoofing artifacts without requiring multimodal fusion. Evaluations on the UniAttackData benchmark demonstrate that DINO-VPT achieves higher accuracy than state-of-the-art VLM-based methods. Our results indicate that a properly structured vision-only architecture can achieve state-of-the-art performance in unified FAS without the need for multimodal supervision.

</details>


### [20] [Sidewalk Moments: Are Richer Representations Always More Human-Aligned? Evidence from City-Walk Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20903)
*Liu Liu,Freya Huying Tan,Fábio Duarte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究挑战了“更丰富的视觉表征必然更符合人类对齐”的假设，发现时间平均图像（TAIs）在二元分类任务中与视频特征表现相当甚至更优，且符合人类判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验更丰富的视觉表征是否能产生更符合人类对齐的城市参与度度量，特别是在常用的二元高/低参与度分类范式下。

Method: 使用61个第一人称城市漫步视频，分割为5万多个10秒片段，通过时空视频特征、时间平均图像（TAIs）、音频嵌入和文本语义描述四种模态进行表示；采用Spearman相关性分析、二元分类模型训练及Amazon Mechanical Turk上的独立二选一强制选择人工评估实验。

Result: 连续对齐中视频特征最强；但在二元分类中，TAIs匹配或优于视频特征，且人工实验证实人类从TAIs和视频中的识别准确率相当，而文本和音频表现较差。功能差异分析显示：动态活动场景适合视频特征，稳定空间结构场景适合TAIs。

Conclusion: 更丰富的表征并非 inherently 更符合人类对齐；基于感知基础的时序压缩（如TAIs）可作为全视频编码的原则性替代方案。

Abstract: We examine whether richer visual representations yield more human-aligned measures of urban engagement, using 61 first-person city-walk videos from YouTube segmented into over 50,000 ten-second clips and represented across four modalities: spatiotemporal video features, temporally averaged images (TAIs), audio embeddings, and text-based semantic descriptions. Spearman correlation analysis reveals the expected ordering along the temporal-richness continuum, with video features showing the strongest continuous alignment. However, this ordering breaks down under binary classification of high- versus low-engagement moments (the paradigm most commonly used to train perceptual scoring models), where TAIs consistently match or outperform video across most classifiers and quantile thresholds. An independent two-alternative forced-choice study on Amazon Mechanical Turk confirms that this parity reflects human judgment: participants identified engaging moments with comparable accuracy from TAIs and full video clips, while text performed substantially worse and audio remained near chance. Gap analysis reveals a functional dissociation: video features are advantaged in activity-driven scenes with dynamic content, whereas TAIs better align with human judgments in composition-driven scenes dominated by stable spatial structure. These findings challenge the assumption that richer representations are inherently more human-aligned, and suggest that perceptually grounded temporal compression can be a principled alternative to full video encoding.

</details>


### [21] [FA-LAM: Focus-Aware Large Avatar Model for One-Shot 4D Animatable Gaussian Head](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20922)
*Yingdong Hu,Yisheng He,Yiming Jiang,Zehong Lin,Steven Hoi,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FA-LAM模型，通过注意力正则化和双阶段训练解决3D头部重建与动画生成的冲突，实现高质量单镜头可动画高斯头部生成及多视角4D流式重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进方法在3D全头生成质量上存在不足，主要受限于错误的注意力激活以及重建与动画任务之间的目标冲突。

Method: 1. 引入对称语义注意力正则化策略，利用人脸的结构对称性纠正噪声注意力；2. 开发双阶段训练管道，分离大视角幻觉和动画能力以解耦任务；3. 结合自回归修改和可见性感知Token融合，支持高效内存友好的多视角流式4D重建。

Result: FA-LAM能够重建出高质量的 animatable Gaussian 全头模型，特别是在细微面部区域和大视角下表现优异，同时支持静态3D和动态4D恢复。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了注意力机制噪声和任务冲突问题，显著提升了全头生成质量和多视角重建效率。

Abstract: We propose FA-LAM, a Focus-Aware Large Avatar Model for one-shot animatable Gaussian head creation, while simultaneously enabling static 3D and dynamic 4D full-head recovery. The core of our method lies in a thorough analysis of the attention mechanisms and the entangled reconstruction and animation training pipeline adopted by prior state-of-the-art approaches. Our analysis identifies two main factors that compromise the quality of 3D full-head generation: (1) incorrect and noisy attention activations, and (2) conflicts between the tasks of reconstruction and animation. To address the first issue, we introduce a symmetric and semantic attention regularization strategy that leverages the inherent semantics and structural symmetry of human heads. To disentangle the objectives of reconstruction and animation, we develop a novel dual-phase training pipeline that separates the model's capabilities for large-view hallucination and animation into distinct modules. Moreover, we enhance our model to support multi-view and streaming 4D reconstruction in an efficient and memory-friendly manner through a core autoregressive modification with tailored visibility-aware token fusion. Collectively, these innovations enable FA-LAM to reconstruct animatable Gaussian full heads with superior quality, particularly in fine facial regions and large viewing angles.

</details>


### [22] [MagicMakeup: A Region-Controllable Diffusion Transformer for High-Fidelity Makeup-Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20924)
*Ziyi Wang,Siming Zheng,Yang Yang,Shusong Xu,Hao Zhang,Bo Li,Changqing Zou,Peng-Tao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MagicMakeup框架，通过Token-Aligned Region Gating和Cross-Modal Perception Guidance解决扩散模型在妆容迁移中的区域控制、属性解耦及高分辨率数据缺失问题，实现高保真且身份保持的区域可控妆容迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的全脸编辑方法在强区域控制性、妆容保真度和身份保持方面仍面临挑战，主要原因为：(i) 像素与注意力机制错位导致非目标区域溢出；(ii) 双图像条件下转移/保留概念分离不清，导致妆容属性与身份耦合；(iii) 缺乏用于细粒度监督的高分辨率、身份一致且带区域标注的数据集。

Method: 提出基于Diffusion Transformer的MagicMakeup框架：1. Token-Aligned Region Gating：将像素掩码与注意力对齐并应用区域特定的logit门控，以实现精确的区域编辑和身份保持；2. Cross-Modal Perception Guidance：通过对齐文本和图像特征来增强跨模态概念感知，明确转移与保留的概念界限；3. 构建1024x1024分辨率的数据对生成管道（通过特定区域妆容去除）及统一基准测试。

Result: 广泛的定量和定性实验表明，MagicMakeup显著提升了区域控制性、妆容保真度和身份保持能力，并在不同风格、种族和姿态下表现出强大的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MagicMakeup通过空间约束和概念解耦有效解决了妆容迁移中的关键难题，为高精度、高保真的面部妆容编辑提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Makeup-transfer applies the reference makeup to the source face while preserving the source identity. Despite advances in full-face editing by diffusion-based methods, strong regional controllability, makeup fidelity, and identity preservation remain challenging. The reasons are (i) pixel-to-attention misalignment that causes spillover into non-target areas and weakens regional control; (ii) unclear transfer/preservation concept separation under two-image conditioning, leading to coupling between makeup attributes and identity; and (iii) the lack of a high-resolution dataset that is identity-consistent and region-labeled for fine-grained supervision. In this paper, we propose MagicMakeup, a diffusion transformer-based framework for region-controllable and high-fidelity makeup transfer, built on spatial constraints and concept disentanglement. To enable precise region-specific editing while preserving identity, we propose Token-Aligned Region Gating, which aligns pixel masks with attention and applies region-specific logit gating. To clarify the concepts of transfer and preservation, we further introduce Cross-Modal Perception Guidance, which aligns text and image features to enhance cross-modal concept perception. We also design a pipeline for the generation of 1024 x 1024 data pairs through region-specific makeup removal and establish a unified benchmark in synthetic and real settings. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments show that MagicMakeup improves regional controllability, makeup fidelity, and identity preservation, with strong robustness across styles, races, and poses.

</details>


### [23] [Ms. Forcing: Efficient Streaming Video Generation with Multi-Scale Patchification and Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20940)
*Zekun Li,Xiaoyan Cong,Hongyu Li,Zhiyang Dou,Chuan Guo,Abhay Mittal,Sizhe An,Srinath Sridhar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Ms.Forcing，一种通过自适应空间粒度优化流式视频生成的新范式，利用多尺度补丁化和自注意力机制显著降低计算成本，并在保持静态计算图的同时提升生成速度和视频质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的流式视频扩散模型受限于嵌套的自回归和去噪循环，难以实时部署；近期的滚动窗口方法虽提升了吞吐量，但因对所有状态使用相同的细粒度空间分词，导致联合去噪窗口中存在大量与噪声相关的冗余。

Method: 1. 多尺度补丁化（MSP）：根据状态噪声水平分配更粗的补丁，减少45%的活动窗口令牌数量；2. 多尺度自注意力（MSSA）：匹配查询尺度的可见非吸收键值密度以进一步降低注意力成本；3. 同质噪声级DMD（H-DMD）：组装共享相同源噪声级别的干净预测来构建假视频，减少训练序列与推理 rollout 之间的不匹配。

Result: 在单张H200 GPU上达到22.84 FPS，比Rolling Forcing快39.6%；在短视频和长视频生成的VBench评分中均有显著提升。

Conclusion: Ms.Forcing通过固定窗位置的调度策略保持了硬件友好的静态计算图，有效解决了传统方法的冗余问题，实现了高效、高速且高质量的流式视频生成。

Abstract: Streaming video diffusion models have made substantial progress toward interactive and dynamic world simulation, but the nested autoregressive and denoising loops of conventional next-frame generation hinder real-time deployment. Recent rolling-window methods pipeline denoising across multiple consecutive frames at different noise levels, improving throughput and long-horizon stability. However, they tokenize every state at the same fine spatial granularity, leaving substantial noise-dependent redundancy in the joint denoising window. We propose Ms.Forcing, an efficient streaming video generation paradigm that adapts spatial granularity to each state's noise level. Its Multi-Scale Patchification (MSP) assigns coarser patches to noisier states, reducing the active-window token count by 45%, while Multi-Scale Self-Attention (MSSA) matches the density of visible non-sink keys and values to each query scale to further reduce attention cost. Because both schedules are fixed by window position, Ms.Forcing retains a static, hardware-friendly computation graph. We further introduce Homogeneous-Noise-Level DMD (H-DMD), which assembles each fake video from clean predictions sharing the same source noise level, thereby reducing the mismatch between DMD training sequences and inference-time rollouts. The multi-scale design helps offset the additional training cost of backpropagating through overlapping windows. We include both quantitative and qualitative experiments to show that Ms.Forcing reaches 22.84 FPS on a single H200 GPU, 39.6% faster than Rolling Forcing, while significantly improving VBench scores in both short video and long video generation setting.

</details>


### [24] [RECO: Region-Aware Compensation for Extrinsic Perturbations in Roadside 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20947)
*Junsheng Du,Zhaocheng He,Yuhuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RECO框架，通过区域感知的6-DoF位姿偏移校正相机外参，解决3D目标检测中因外参误差导致的特征错位和定位退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路边3D目标检测方法对外参（extrinsics）高度敏感，轻微偏差经投影几何放大后会导致严重的特征错位和定位性能下降。

Method: 1. 构建区域感知的外参补偿框架（RECO），利用可学习的范围边界将场景分为近/远区域并估计特定区域的位姿修正；2. 使用可微Sigmoid门控平滑融合两种补偿几何以确保持续的BEV采样；3. 引入辅助重投影损失，通过比较3D GT投影的2D边界框与2D标注来监督外参优化。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X-I和Rope3D基准测试中，针对偏航角（yaw）和Z轴偏差下的外参扰动，RECO相比最先进基线方法表现出一致的性能提升，并能从瞬态扰动泛化至持续偏移，在校准不确定性严格条件下保持极具竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: RECO有效缓解了外参敏感性问题，显著提升了智能交通系统中3D目标检测在存在相机标定误差时的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: In intelligent transportation systems, roadside 3D object detection provides wide-area perception crucial for traffic understanding, cooperative early warning, and safe autonomous driving. However, existing methods suffer from high sensitivity to camera extrinsics; even slight deviations (whether manifesting as transient jitter or persistent drift) can be significantly amplified by projective geometry. This cascade results in severe feature misalignment and degraded localization. To mitigate this limitation, we propose RECO, a region-aware extrinsic compensation framework that corrects extrinsics using piecewise 6-DoF pose offsets. RECO predicts a learnable range boundary to partition the scene into near and far regions, estimating region-specific pose corrections. A differentiable sigmoid gate then smoothly blends the two compensated geometries to preserve continuous BEV sampling and facilitate stable optimization. To supervise the refinement of extrinsics, we introduce an auxiliary reprojection loss that compares 2D bounding boxes projected from 3D ground truth against 2D annotations, optimizing it jointly with the standard detection objective. Extensive experiments on the DAIR-V2X-I and Rope3D benchmarks under extrinsic perturbations demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines across both yaw and $z$-axis deviations. RECO also generalizes from transient perturbations to persistent shifts, maintaining highly competitive performance under strict calibration uncertainty.

</details>


### [25] [FSB-Net: Frequency-Spatial Boundary Network for Brain Stroke Lesion Segmentation in Non-Contrast CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20955)
*Linke Fan,Xianglong Li,Huixin Huang,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FSB-Net，一种利用频域分析进行边界感知脑卒中病变分割的频率-空间边界网络，通过小波边界检测、频空交叉注意力和谱边界损失解决NCCT图像中病灶边界模糊和对比度低的问题，在公共数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非增强CT（NCCT）扫描中脑卒中病灶分割面临低对比度、异质性形态及部分容积效应导致的边界模糊等挑战；现有深度学习方法缺乏显式边界建模，导致分割不精确，影响体积评估和治疗计划制定。

Method: 构建FSB-Net框架，基于PVTv2-B2编码器，包含三个核心组件：(1) 小波边界检测头（WBDH），利用离散小波变换提取多尺度特征的高频子带作为边界表示；(2) 频空交叉注意力模块（FSCAM），在波边界特征与空间解码器特征间执行双向注意力以增强边界；(3) 谱边界损失，在傅里叶域惩罚高频差异以优化边界锐度。

Result: 在包含缺血性和出血性病例的公共脑卒中CT数据集上，FSB-Net在平均Dice系数、平均IoU和HD95指标上均优于U-Net、UNet++、MANet和DeepLabV3+，实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 引入频域分析和显式边界建模能显著提升NCCT脑卒中病灶分割的精度，FSB-Net为处理边界模糊和低对比度问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate segmentation of brain stroke lesions in non-contrast computed tomography (NCCT) scans is critical for rapid clinical decision-making, yet remains difficult due to the low contrast between lesion and normal brain tissue, heterogeneous lesion morphology across ischemic and hemorrhagic subtypes, and ambiguous boundaries caused by partial volume effects. Current deep learning approaches primarily optimize region-level overlap but lack explicit boundary modeling, leading to imprecise delineation that can affect volumetric assessment and treatment planning. We propose FSB-Net, a frequency-spatial boundary network that leverages frequency-domain analysis for boundary-aware stroke lesion segmentation. FSB-Net introduces three components: (i) a Wavelet Boundary Detection Head (WBDH) that applies the discrete wavelet transform to multi-scale encoder features, extracting high-frequency sub-bands as boundary representations; (ii) a Frequency-Spatial Cross-Attention Module (FSCAM) that performs bidirectional attention between wavelet boundary features and spatial decoder features for selective boundary enhancement; and (iii) a Spectral Boundary Loss that penalizes high-frequency discrepancies in the Fourier domain to optimize boundary sharpness. Built on a PVTv2-B2 encoder, FSB-Net is evaluated on a public Brain Stroke CT dataset containing both ischemic and hemorrhagic cases. Experimental results show that FSB-Net outperforms U-Net, UNet++, MANet, and DeepLabV3+ across all metrics, achieving state-of-the-art performance in mean Dice, mean IoU, and HD95.

</details>


### [26] [Unsupervised Metal Artifact Reduction in Dental CBCT using Fine-tuned Cycle-Consistent Adversarial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20977)
*G. L. T. Chamika,S. N. A. Dhanapala,P. H. S. V. Nimalaweera,Maheshi B. Dissanayake,Ruwan D. Jayasinghe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CycleGAN的无监督深度学习框架，用于减少牙科种植体在CBCT成像中的金属伪影。该方法利用约4000张非配对图像进行训练，显著提升了图像质量指标（BRISQUE提升34.6%，FID降低至157.04，SSIM达0.9105），并实现了每切片3.03毫秒的实时推理效率，旨在作为临床决策支持工具以提高诊断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科种植体产生的金属伪影严重降低了锥形束计算机断层扫描（CBCT）图像的质量，遮蔽了关键的解剖结构，从而损害了诊断的精确性。现有的监督学习方法依赖于难以获得的体素对齐配对数据集，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于循环一致性对抗网络（CycleGAN）的无监督深度学习框架。该架构集成了基于U-Net的生成器和PatchGAN判别器，专门针对减轻生成幻觉和保持形态完整性进行了优化。模型使用来自公共ToothFairy数据集的非配对数据集（约4,000张图像）进行训练，无需配对数据即可实现高保真恢复。

Result: 在保留的测试集上的定量基准测试显示：盲参考图像空间质量评估器（BRISQUE）分数提高了34.6%；弗雷歇起始距离（FID）从207.03大幅降至157.04；结构相似性指数测量值（SSIM）达到0.9105的高分。推理速度为每切片3.03毫秒，实现了实时效率。专家验证确认了高保真度，但在极端情况下建议在人机协作监督下使用。

Conclusion: 该框架通过可扩展的软件管道增强了诊断清晰度，为高保真牙科种植体成像提供了稳健的解决方案。作为一种临床决策支持工具，它在抑制伪影的同时保留了详细的解剖结构，有助于提高CBCT成像的诊断可靠性。

Abstract: Metal artifacts generated by dental implants significantly degrade cone-beam computed tomography (CBCT) volumes, obscuring critical anatomical structures and compromising diagnostic precision. To address this, an unsupervised deep learning framework has been proposed for Metal Artifact Reduction (MAR) utilizing a Cycle-Consistent Adversarial Network (CycleGAN) optimized for high-fidelity restoration. Unlike supervised methods that rely on unattainable voxel-aligned paired datasets, the proposed approach leverages an unpaired dataset of approximately 4,000 images, curated from the public ToothFairy dataset. The architecture integrates U-Net-based generators and PatchGAN discriminators, specifically tuned to mitigate generative hallucinations and preserve morphological integrity. Quantitative benchmarking on a held-out test set demonstrates a 34.6\% improvement in the Blind/Referenceless Image Spatial Quality Evaluator (BRISQUE) score, a substantial reduction in Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) from 207.03 to 157.04, and a superior Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM) of 0.9105. The framework achieves real-time efficiency with a 3.03 ms inference time per slice, effectively suppressing artifacts while preserving anatomical detail. Expert validation confirms high fidelity; however, to ensure reliability in extreme cases, the architecture is recommended as a clinical decision-support tool under human-in-the-loop oversight. By enhancing diagnostic clarity via a scalable software pipeline, this study provides a robust solution for high-fidelity dental implant imaging.

</details>


### [27] [Distribution-Alignment Bridge for Uncertainty-Aware Text-to-Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20984)
*Kyeongmo Chae,Jihoon Lee,Sangtae Ahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出分布对齐桥（DAB）框架，将文本到视频检索重构为分布对齐任务，通过高斯分布建模不确定性，利用扩散启发的桥接机制迭代优化，并在多个基准上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性点匹配方法未能有效处理模态间的特异性不确定性，难以充分捕捉文本与视频嵌入之间的复杂分布差异。

Method: 1. 将文本和视频嵌入建模为由均值和方差定义的高斯分布；2. 采用确定性、受扩散启发的桥接机制，通过截断细化过程将文本分布迭代细化至目标视频分布；3. 引入基于KL散度的分布感知对比损失以优化跨模态相似度；4. 构建端到端可训练系统，统一概率嵌入与分布变换。

Result: 在MSR-VTT、MSVD和VATEX基准上的广泛评估表明，DAB显著优于现有的概率和基于扩散的基线模型，并通过桥接诱导的分布边际提供校准后的不确定性感知排序。

Conclusion: DAB框架通过将检索问题转化为分布对齐任务，有效整合了概率嵌入与分布变换，提升了跨模态检索性能及不确定性估计能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes the Distribution-Alignment Bridge (DAB), a framework that reconceptualizes text-to-video retrieval as a distribution alignment task rather than traditional deterministic point matching. By modeling both text and video embeddings as Gaussian distributions defined by mean and variance, DAB explicitly accounts for modality-specific uncertainty. We employ a deterministic, diffusion-inspired bridge to iteratively refine text distributions toward their target video distributions through a truncated refinement process. This approach unifies probabilistic embedding and distributional transformation into a cohesive, end-to-end trainable system. To optimize cross-modal similarity, we introduce a distribution-aware contrastive loss based on Kullback-Leibler divergence. Extensive evaluations on MSR-VTT, MSVD, and VATEX benchmarks confirm that DAB significantly outperforms existing probabilistic and diffusion-based baselines, while providing calibrated uncertainty-aware ranking through bridge-induced distributional margins.

</details>


### [28] [HyWorldVLA: A Vision-Language-Action Model with Hybrid World Modeling for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20988)
*Quanfu Yu,Xian Wu,Hao Xu,Liulong Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HyWorldVLA，一种结合像素级监督与潜在表示学习的混合世界模型-VLA框架，旨在解决自动驾驶中像素预测鲁棒性差和纯潜在模型可解释性低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于像素的未来预测在噪声驾驶场景中鲁棒性不足，而基于潜在的世界模型虽能缓解敏感性，但缺乏像素级 grounding 导致可解释性受限及表征退化。需调和这一权衡。

Method: 1. 预训练阶段：同时预测由预训练视频VAE编码的视频潜在变量并重建视频帧，以提供精确的像素级 grounding；2. 协同微调阶段：仅预测潜在特征，并将其输入动作专家生成轨迹。

Result: 在NAVSIM v1和v2基准测试中，HyWorldVLA显著优于基于像素和基于潜在的基线模型。首次对自动驾驶世界模型的噪声鲁棒性进行了全面的定性和定量分析。

Conclusion: HyWorldVLA通过统一像素级监督和潜在表示学习，有效平衡了细粒度时空推理与鲁棒性，为评估未来架构建立了新基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models augmented with world modeling represent a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving. While pixel-level future prediction enables fine-grained spatiotemporal reasoning, it compromises robustness in noisy driving scenarios. Conversely, latent-based world models alleviate this sensitivity but often incur limited interpretability and representational degradation due to absent pixel-level grounding. To reconcile this trade-off, we propose HyWorldVLA, a hybrid world-VLA framework that unifies pixel-level supervision and latent representation learning. In the pre-training stage, HyWorldVLA predicts video latents encoded by a pre-trained video VAE, while simultaneously reconstructing video frames to provide precise pixel-level grounding. During the subsequent co-fine-tuning phase, the model exclusively predicts latent features, which are fed into an action expert to generate trajectories. Extensive experiments on NAVSIM v1 and v2 benchmarks demonstrate that HyWorldVLA significantly outperforms both pixel-based and latent-based world model baselines. Notably, we present the first comprehensive qualitative and quantitative analysis of world model noise robustness in autonomous driving, establishing a new benchmark for evaluating future architectures.

</details>


### [29] [Latent Variable-Mediated Cross-Learning for Few-Shot Acoustic Impedance Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20989)
*Junheng Peng,Yong Li,Mingwei Wang,Yi Bao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RD-SCL框架，结合正则化反卷积与半监督交叉学习，通过可微分Tikhonov反算子动态估计地震子波，解决声阻抗成像中标签稀缺和先验不准确的问题，在SEAM和Marmousi 2基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声阻抗成像是严重不适定的问题，面临地震子波未知、观测频带受限及标签样本极度稀缺（通常<1%）的挑战。现有半监督深度学习方法依赖不准确的先验子波假设或引入辅助网络，导致优化不稳定和性能下降。

Method: 提出RD-SCL框架，核心包括：1）基于频域动态估计潜在子波的闭式一阶Tikhonov正则化反卷积算子，提供物理引导的稳定反馈；2）对称交叉学习机制，强制标注数据与未标注数据预测的一致性，充分利用大量未标注迹线。

Result: 在SEAM和Marmousi 2基准测试中，RD-SCL consistently优于最先进的监督和半监督方法，取得显著性能提升，同时计算成本更低。模型仅含56.5k可学习参数，运行时间具有竞争力。

Conclusion: RD-SCL提供了一种实用、物理一致且高效的声阻抗成像解决方案，有效缓解少样本问题，无需固定子波先验或辅助网络。

Abstract: Acoustic impedance imaging is a fundamental yet severely ill-posed problem in subsurface analysis: the seismic wavelet is unknown, observations are band-limited, and labeled well-log samples are extremely scarce (typically <1% of all traces). Existing semi-supervised deep learning methods mitigate few-shot problem by incorporating forward modeling, yet they either rely on inaccurate prior wavelet assumptions or introduce auxiliary networks, leading to unstable optimization and degraded performance. We propose RD-SCL, a novel framework that integrates regularized deconvolution with semi-supervised cross-learning. At its core lies a differentiable, closed-form first-order Tikhonov deconvolution operator that dynamically estimates the latent wavelet in the frequency domain during training, providing stable physics-guided feedback without explicit auxiliary networks and fixed wavelet priors. Building on this operator, we design a symmetric cross-learning that enforces consistency between predictions on labeled and unlabeled data, thereby effectively exploiting abundant unlabeled traces. Extensive experiments on the SEAM and Marmousi 2 benchmarks demonstrate that RD-SCL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art supervised and semi-supervised methods, achieving substantial gains with lower computational cost. With only 56.5k learnable parameters and competitive runtime, RD-SCL offers a practical, physically consistent, and efficient solution for acoustic impedance imaging.

</details>


### [30] [Sparse Concept Channels in Frozen 3D CT Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20993)
*Farhad Nooralahzadeh,Lea Bogensperger,Christian Bluethgen,Michael Krauthammer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种免训练的“概念通道探针”（CCP）方法，揭示了3D医学视觉-语言模型中冻结视觉编码器对临床发现的稀疏编码机制，并在跨模型泛化和生成指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型视觉-语言模型在3D医学图像解释中占据主导地位，但尚不清楚哪些内部单元编码了临床发现，以及这些信息在表示中的具体位置。

Method: 1. 使用冻结的视觉嵌入对3D胸部视觉-语言模型（Pillar-0）进行探测研究；2. 开发免训练的概念通道探针（CCP）方法，结合语料库衍生的报告模板；3. 验证该方法在架构无关的3D腹部VLM（Merlin）上的泛化能力；4. 与CT-CHAT进行临床疗效和自然语言生成（NLG）指标对比。

Result: 1. 每个放射学发现由约10个稀疏匹配的视觉编码器通道编码，性能媲美全特征分类且远超零样本文本提示；关闭相关通道会导致对应评分崩溃而无关标签稳定；2. 相同的稀疏探针在腹部VLM上复现，证明这是冻结医学编码器的通用属性；3. CCP方法在临床疗效（F1 0.549 vs 0.184）和NLG指标（BLEU 0.483 vs 0.373）上优于CT-CHAT，且延迟降低22倍。

Conclusion: 研究提供了冻结医学编码器如何表示发现的清晰、可复现的特征描述，证明了其直接适用于不同模型，并展示了高效且高性能的替代方案。

Abstract: Large vision-language models are becoming increasingly dominant in 3D medical image interpretation, but we rarely know <i>which</i> internal units encode clinical findings or <i>where</i> that information lives in the representation. We first study this on a 3D chest vision-language model (Pillar-0) by probing its frozen vision embeddings. We show that (i) each radiological finding is encoded by a <i>sparse</i> set of ~10 vision-encoder channels that match full-feature classification performance and far exceed a zero-shot text prompting; (ii) turning off the channels tied to one finding, that finding's score collapses while unrelated labels stay stable; and (iii) the same sparse probe <i>replicates</i> on an architecturally unrelated 3D abdominal VLM (Merlin) suggesting a general property of frozen medical encoders. Our training-free concept channel probe (CCP) method, paired with a corpus-derived report template, outperforms published CT-CHAT on clinical efficacy and NLG metrics (F1 0.549 vs. 0.184; BLEU 0.483 vs. 0.373) at 22x lower latency. Our results provide a clear, reproducible characterization of how frozen medical encoders represent findings, demonstrating direct applicability across models.

</details>


### [31] [AUCH-Net: Action Unit-Based Consistency-Aware Hypergraph Network for Cross-Domain Few-Shot Facial Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21004)
*Xinhan Qiu,Yan Yan,Rui Zhu,Si Chen,Hanzi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动作单元（AU）的一致性感知超图网络（AUCH-Net），用于解决跨域少样本面部表情识别（CF-FER）中因领域差异大和目标样本有限导致的特征迁移困难问题。该方法通过构建AU一致性超图，结合AU特征学习和视觉特征学习模块，有效 bridging 细粒度面部变化与高层表情类别之间的差距，并在多个数据集上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨域少样本面部表情识别（CF-FER）方法在面临较大的领域差异和有限的目标域样本时，难以学习到具有良好迁移性的特征，导致性能不佳。然而，动作单元（AU）作为描述面部肌肉运动的指标，在不同域内和域间提供了概念语义上的一致性，这为解决上述问题提供了新的思路。

Method: 提出了动作单元一致性感知超图网络（AUCH-Net）。该网络包含两个核心模块：1) AU特征学习（AFL）模块，在关系一致性损失和AU正则化损失的指导下学习AU特征；2) 视觉特征学习（VFL）模块，在关系一致性损失和分类损失的监督下学习视觉特征。通过利用新颖的关系一致性损失构建一致性感知超图，模型能够有效地对AU与表情类别之间的连接进行建模。

Result: 在实验室环境（in-the-lab）和真实世界场景（in-the-wild）的多个数据集上进行了广泛实验。结果表明，AUCH-Net consistently outperforms several state-of-the-art methods（持续优于多种最先进的方法）。结果证实，在跨域少样本场景下，对AU之间的关系进行建模具有巨大的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过利用动作单元（AU）提供的一致性语义，AUCH-Net成功建立了细粒度面部变化与高层表情类别之间的联系，显著促进了可迁移特征表示的学习。该方法证明了在跨域少样本面部表情识别任务中，基于AU关系的建模是提升性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Recently, cross-domain few-shot facial expression recognition (CF-FER) has received considerable attention. However, the performance of existing CF-FER methods is still unsatisfactory due to inferior transferable feature learning under large domain discrepancy and limited target samples. Fortunately, the action units (AUs), which indicate the movements of different facial muscles, provide consistent conceptual semantics for describing expressions within and across domains. Inspired by this, we propose a novel Action Unit-based Consistency-aware Hypergraph Network (AUCH-Net), which constructs consistency-aware hypergraphs on AUs, for CF-FER. Specifically, AUCH-Net presents a new AU feature learning (AFL) module and a new visual feature learning (VFL) module. The AFL module learns AU features under the guidance of a novel relation consistency loss and an AU regularization loss, while the VFL module learns visual features supervised by a relation consistency loss and a classification loss. By learning consistent AU features, AUCH-Net effectively models the connections between AUs and expression categories. As a result, we can bridge the gap between fine-grained facial variations and high-level expression categories, greatly facilitating the learning of transferable feature representations.Extensive experiments on both in-the-lab and in-the-wild datasets show that our method consistently outperforms several state-of-the-art methods. Our results clearly show that modeling the relationships among AUs holds significant potential for FER under cross-domain few-shot scenarios.

</details>


### [32] [Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21007)
*Abhijeet Narang,Kartik Kuckreja,Shreya Ghosh,Muhammad Haris Khan,Usman Tariq,Jianfei Cai,Abhinav Dhall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACM Multimedia 2026举办的可解释Deepfake检测挑战赛，基于百万级XPlainVerse基准测试，要求参赛者在分类真假的同时，为技术用户和一般用户提供两种不同复杂度的自然语言解释，并通过分类指标及语义相似度、简洁性、意图感知接地等指标综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界验证场景中， diverse用户不仅需要知道图像是否被篡改，还需要理解为何被视为可疑，因此需要系统从二元分类转向能提供视觉证据解释的可解释检测系统。

Method: 构建基于XPlainVerse基准的挑战赛，要求参与者提交真实/虚假标签以及针对每张图片的两类解释（详细复杂版供技术人员使用，简洁简单版供普通用户阅读），并采用结合分类指标与语义相似度、简洁性和意图感知接地指标的综合评估体系。

Result: 该挑战赛旨在benchmark联合能力（分类+解释生成），评估方法是否能识别相关篡改实体并提供支持的视觉证据，相关评估脚本、基线模型和代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过该挑战赛开发的方法论将有助于推动下一代可解释Deepfake检测器的发展。

Abstract: Deepfake detection is moving beyond binary classification decisions toward systems that can also explain the visual evidence supporting those decisions. This transition is important for real-world verification settings, where diverse users need to understand not only whether an image is manipulated, but also why it is considered suspicious. The Explainable Deepfake Detection Challenge at ACM Multimedia 2026 is designed to benchmark this joint capability. Built on XPlainVerse, a million-scale benchmark for explainable deepfake detection, the challenge evaluates methods on image classification and grounded natural-language explanation generation. Participants submit a real/fake label together with two explanations for each image: a detailed complex explanation for technical users and a concise simple explanation for general users. The evaluation combines classification metrics with semantic similarity, simplicity, and intent-aware grounding metrics that assess whether explanations identify the relevant manipulated entities and supporting visual evidence. The methodologies developed through the challenge will contribute to the development of next-generation explainable deepfake detectors. Evaluation script, baseline models, and accompanying code are available on https://github.com/Abhijeet8901/XPlainVerse-ACMChallenge.

</details>


### [33] [ProCap: Prominence-guided Object Rectification for Faithful and Comprehensive Video Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21022)
*Debjyoti Das Adhikary,Aritra Hazra,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重训的显著性感知迭代后处理框架，通过排序和多次提示注入缺失对象，显著提升视频描述的质量与完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频描述方法要么依赖昂贵的模型重训，要么仅进行单次固定修正且未优先处理关键对象，导致语义重要内容遗漏或幻觉问题。

Method: 构建一个轻量级评分机制，根据空间显著性、时间持久性和关系动态对检测到的对象进行排名；随后通过迭代式的提示驱动优化循环，按优先级逐步将缺失但相关的对象注入到描述中，且不修改底层模型参数。

Result: 在MSVD和MSR-VTT数据集上验证，相比强预训练基线，人类评估显示感知完整性最高提升48%，幻觉减少45%；该方法无需重训或参考描述，效果优于ChatGPT和Gemini等对比模型。

Conclusion: 该基于显著性引导的迭代校正方法是一种轻量级、可扩展且模型无关的方案，能有效提高视频描述的完整性和可信度，适用于无障碍访问、检索等多媒体理解应用。

Abstract: Improving video captioning quality typically demands retraining large vision-language models, an expensive and often impractical requirement. Existing training-free alternatives instead ground captions in detected objects to curb hallucination, but apply only a single, fixed correction pass without prioritizing which objects matter most, leaving semantically significant content omitted. We propose a prominence-aware, iterative post-hoc rectification framework that overcomes both limitations without modifying the underlying captioning model's parameters: a lightweight scoring mechanism ranks detected objects by spatial saliency, temporal persistence, and relational dynamics, and an iterative, prompt-driven refinement loop uses this ranking to progressively inject missing yet contextually relevant objects into the caption over multiple rounds. We validate the framework on MSVD and MSR-VTT using object-grounded automatic metrics, a 110-participant human study, and qualitative comparison against ChatGPT and Gemini; in human evaluation, the framework raises perceived completeness by up to 48% and reduces hallucination by up to 45% relative to a strong pretrained captioning baseline, all without retraining or reference captions. These results position prominence-guided iterative rectification as a lightweight, scalable, and model-agnostic route to more complete and trustworthy video captioning, with direct relevance to accessibility, retrieval, and other multimedia understanding applications.

</details>


### [34] [WAT3R: Feedforward Underwater 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21023)
*Jiayi Xu,Jiahao Lu,Ziqiang Zheng,Yihao Tan,Yaolong Zhu,Yuan Liu,Sai-Kit Yeung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WAT3R，一种基于前向传播的水下3D重建框架，通过轻量级神经适应模块解决光线衰减和后向散射问题，直接从水下视频输出高精度3D点图和相机位姿，在多个数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下环境中的严重光线衰减和后向散射会降低视觉质量并破坏视图间特征一致性，导致多视图几何重建不准确，现有的可靠前向水下3D重建方法仍面临挑战。

Method: 提出WAT3R框架，将退化适应作为几何约束过程，集成轻量级神经适应模块以灵活处理水下成像效应；该框架通过单次前向传递，直接从水下视频中输出像素对齐的3D点图和相机位姿。

Result: 在FLSea、SQUID和USOD10K数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多视图/单目深度估计和相机位姿估计等3D重建任务中，一致优于最先进的方法。

Conclusion: WAT3R通过结合退化适应与几何约束，实现了高效且高质量的水下3D重建，为水下视觉应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable feedforward underwater 3D reconstruction remains challenging due to severe light attenuation and backscattering, which degrade visual quality and disrupt feature consistency across views, leading to inaccurate multi-view geometry. To address this issue, we propose WAT3R, a feed-forward framework for reconstructing 3D scenes directly from underwater images. By leveraging degradation adaptation as a geometry-constrained process, WAT3R integrates a lightweight neural adaptation module to flexibly account for these underwater imaging effects, thereby improving multi-view reconstruction quality. Implemented in a single forward pass, WAT3R directly and efficiently outputs pixel-aligned 3D point maps and camera poses from underwater videos, allowing a high-quality underwater 3D reconstruction. Experiments conducted on the FLSea, SQUID, and USOD10K datasets show that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches on 3D reconstruction tasks, including multi-view/monocular depth estimation and camera pose estimation.

</details>


### [35] [GroupVideo: Multi-Identity Customized Text-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21027)
*Xinyang Song,Libin Wang,Jianxin Sun,Qi Li,Dandan Zheng,JingDong Chen,Zhenan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GroupVideo框架，解决多身份视频生成中的身份混淆和“复制粘贴”现象。通过视觉/语义对齐、ID定位模块及新数据集20k视频，实现一致身份与自然动作的多角色视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单身份方法无法处理多身份场景；直接扩展的单身份框架在多身份下导致面部表情/动作不自然（“复制粘贴”现象），且缺乏显式身份分离机制引发身份混淆。

Method: 1. 基于Video Diffusion Transformers的GroupVideo框架；2. 多模态身份对齐：视觉对齐联合编码多张人脸提供鲁棒参考，语义对齐引入语义感知器提升动作自然度；3. ID定位模块：空间引导+边界框约束+掩码正则化损失，聚焦面部区域以提升保真度和训练效率；4. 构建20,000条高质量多身份视频数据集。

Result: 实验表明GroupVideo在生成具有身份一致性和自然动作的多角色视频方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GroupVideo有效解决了多身份视频生成的身份混淆与动作不自然问题，其提出的多模态对齐、ID定位技术及开源数据集为后续研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Current identity customized video generation methodologies are predominantly limited to single-identity scenarios, as the lack of explicit identity separation mechanisms often leads to identity confusion in multi-identity settings. Existing multi-identity approaches, which directly extend single-identity frameworks by concatenating face images as input conditions, frequently result in unnatural facial expressions and motions, manifesting as the "copy-paste" phenomenon. To overcome these limitations, we introduce GroupVideo, a novel framework that leverages multiple individual photographs to generate identitycustomized video. Built upon Video Diffusion Transformers, GroupVideo incorporates multimodal identity alignment: visual alignment jointly encodes multiple face images to provide robust identity references, while semantic alignment introduces a semantic perceiver to enhance the naturalness of motions. An ID localization module with spatial guidance is introduced to address identity blending and enhance identity fidelity, along with bounding box constraints and mask regularization loss, to focus on facial regions and improve training efficiency. In response to the shortage of multi-ID video datasets, we have curated a comprehensive high-quality dataset of 20,000 videos, thereby establishing a crucial resource to advance future research in multi-ID video generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that GroupVideo outperforms existing methods in generating multi-character videos with consistent identities and natural motions.

</details>


### [36] [Spectral-Spatial Synergistic Guided Network for Hyperspectral Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21032)
*Yanyan Peng,Tingfa Xu,Yao Xiao,Peifu Liu,Shuyan Bai,Fengxiang Xu,Jianan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出S3GNet，一种轻量级高效的光谱-空间协同引导网络，通过光谱结构感知模块解决光照干扰问题，利用流感知注意力模块实现光谱空间协作，并采用渐进门控细化解码器优化边界，在计算效率和检测精度上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高光谱显著目标检测方法常混淆由外部因素（如光照）引起的偶然光谱变化与由物体固有材料属性引起的光谱差异，导致特征表示脆弱且预测噪声大。

Method: 提出S3GNet网络，核心为结构感知，构建闭环信息流：1) 引入无参数光谱结构感知模块，利用光谱导数和区域分层建模提取抗光照变化的鲁棒特征；2) 设计流感知注意力模块，通过流间全局交互和流内空间指导实现有效的光谱-空间协作；3) 使用渐进门控细化解码器，通过最优整合多尺度特征确保精确的目标边界和细节恢复。

Result: 实验结果表明，S3GNet在计算效率和检测准确性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: S3GNet通过光谱-空间协同机制有效解决了高光谱显著目标检测中的光照干扰问题，实现了高精度、高效率的检测。

Abstract: Hyperspectral salient object detection aims to identify visually salient regions from hyperspectral images. Existing methods often fail because they fundamentally misunderstand the data, confusing incidental spectral variations caused by external factors such as illumination with essential spectral differences caused by the intrinsic material properties of the object. This leads to fragile representations and noisy predictions. To this end, we propose a lightweight and efficient Spectral-Spatial Synergistic Guided Network (S3GNet), with structure perception as the core, to build a closed-loop information flow around spectrum robust modeling, cross-stream co-perception and multi-scale refinement decoding. S3GNet introduces a parameter-free Spectral Structure-Aware Module that leverages spectral derivatives and regional hierarchical modeling to extract intrinsic features of robustness against illumination variations. Our Stream-Aware Attention Module achieves effective spectral-spatial collaboration through inter-stream global interaction and intra-stream spatial guidance. Furthermore, a Progressive Gated Refinement Decoder ensures precise object boundaries and detail recovery by optimally integrating multi-scale features. Experimental results show that S3GNet achieves superior performance in both computational efficiency and detection accuracy compared to existing methods.

</details>


### [37] [GeoThreat: Transferable Targeted Adversarial Attacks on Large Vision-Language Models for Remote Sensing Image Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21036)
*Yimin Fu,Yuefeng Bai,Baicheng Pan,Zhunga Liu,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoThreat，一种针对遥感图像解释的大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的可迁移定向对抗攻击方法，通过概念和感知层面的联合优化实现有效语义操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM对抗攻击主要关注自然图像，而遥感图像解释需结合局部判别线索与全局场景上下文，在黑盒设置下实现可迁移的语义操控面临更大挑战，且该领域研究尚不充分。

Method: 提出GeoThreat方法：1）使用代理图像编码器的类标记作为概念表示；2）通过协作重要性估计从对抗样本的补丁标记中蒸馏感知表示；3）引入对抗-目标相似性梯度以准确表征局部视觉线索与预期语义操控的相关性；4）通过交叉注意力机制将感知表示动态对齐到目标补丁标记；5）通过集成基础的概念校准和感知适应联合优化迭代更新对抗扰动。

Result: 在多种不同的LVLMs上进行的广泛实验表明，GeoThreat在可迁移性和可控性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GeoThreat有效解决了遥感图像解释中LVLM对抗攻击的挑战，显著提升了攻击的可迁移性和对目标语义的控制能力。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks against large vision-language models (LVLMs) serve as an effective means of assessing their robustness in cross-modal semantic understanding. Existing studies mainly focus on corrupting visual inputs to induce predefined erroneous responses in general vision-language tasks, whereas corresponding investigations in remote sensing fields remain largely underexplored. Compared with natural image understanding, remote sensing image interpretation requires joint reasoning over local discriminative cues and global scene context. This poses additional challenges to achieving transferable semantic manipulation toward specified responses under black-box settings. To tackle these challenges, we propose GeoThreat, a transferable targeted adversarial attack method against LVLMs for remote sensing image interpretation. Specifically, GeoThreat modulates adversarial representations in accordance with the target content at both conceptual and perceptual levels. The class tokens from surrogate image encoders are employed as conceptual representations, while perceptual representations are distilled from patch tokens of the adversarial example through collaborative importance estimation. Beyond merely rolling out attention scores across layers, we incorporate adversarial-target similarity gradients to more faithfully characterize the relevance of local visual cues to the intended semantic manipulation. The perceptual representations are then dynamically aligned with target patch tokens in a cross-attentive manner, facilitating the adaptation of local cues toward designated semantic details.
