Under the same architecture and training protocol, invalid mass increases to 0.155929 in the medium-complexity regime. Hard filtering removes all invalid retained trajectories while preserving 84.4% of generated samples, whereas soft weighting preserves effective sample size but yields only limited validity gains. Family-level analysis shows that dependency constraints account for nearly all observed inadmissibility. These results indicate that statistical plausibility and structural admissibility are distinct reliability properties and that symbolic constraint handling becomes more valuable as graph-structural complexity increases.

</details>


### [289] [Agent-Guided Relational Concept Discovery: Toward Interpretable Surgical Margin Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21437)
*Nooshin Maghsoodi,Amoon Jamzad,Robert Policelli,Mohammad Farahmand,Dilakshan Srikanthan,Martin Kaufmann,Kevin Y. M. Ren,Shaila Merchant,Sonal Varma,Ross Walker,Doug McKay,John Rudan,Gabor Fichtinger,Parvin Mousavi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“Agent-Guided Concept Discovery”的框架，通过无监督的方式从REIMS数据中学习可解释的概念，结合推理代理和生化知识图谱，解决了深度学习在手术边缘评估中泛化能力差和缺乏可解释性的问题，并在皮肤癌和乳腺癌数据集上提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习能有效利用快速蒸发电离质谱（REIMS）数据进行手术边缘评估，但其临床应用受限，主要原因包括：1. 模型在手术室噪声和无标签条件下的泛化能力不足；2. 深度学习模型的“黑盒”性质导致难以理解和改进其决策过程；3. 现有的基于概念的学习方法依赖难以获取的手术场景概念标注。

Method: 提出了“Agent-Guided Concept Discovery”框架：1. 无需预定义概念标签，直接从数据中学习有意义的概念；2. 训练过程中使用推理代理（reasoning agent）优化概念的语义描述，并根据诊断相关性自适应调整权重；3. 利用生化知识图谱对概念进行 grounding（锚定），确保其与已知代谢关系一致，增强生物学合理性。

Result: 在皮肤癌和乳腺癌数据集上，该模型相比基线模型提高了平衡准确率和敏感性；在代表性术中案例中，显示出更少的假阳性结果，表明其对手术条件的泛化能力更强。

Conclusion: 该框架通过引入推理代理和知识图谱约束，实现了无需人工标注的可解释概念学习，有效提升了REIMS数据在复杂手术环境下的分析性能和鲁棒性，为临床部署提供了更有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Deep learning models can effectively use Rapid Evaporative Ionization Mass Spectrometry (REIMS) data for surgical margin assessment. However, their clinical adoption remains challenging due to limited generalization to operating room conditions. This difficulty arises because models are typically trained on labeled spectra collected from resected tissue samples, while they must operate on noisy, unlabeled data acquired directly during surgery. In addition, the black-box nature of deep learning models makes it difficult to understand and systematically improve their behavior. Concept-based learning offers a promising way to address these challenges by mapping raw measurements to human-understandable concepts. However, supervised concept-based approaches rely on concept annotations, which are difficult to obtain in complex mass spectrometry workflows. We propose Agent-Guided Concept Discovery, a framework that learns meaningful concepts directly from data without requiring predefined concept labels. During training, a reasoning agent refines semantic descriptions of the learned concepts and adaptively adjusts their weight based on diagnostic relevance. These concepts are further grounded using a biochemical knowledge graph to ensure consistency with known metabolic relationships. Across Skin and Breast Cancer datasets, our model improves balanced accuracy and sensitivity over the baseline. In a representative intraoperative case, it shows fewer false positives, indicating better generalization to surgical conditions.

</details>


### [290] [Detecting LLM-Generated Tokens in Human--LLM Coauthored Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21458)
*Yangjun Lu,Hongyi Zhou,Fabian Spill,Kai Ye,Chengchun Shi,Jin Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Token级的LLM生成内容定位方法，通过平滑相邻Token得分并采用自适应Lepski规则选择带宽，无需训练数据即可实现混合作者文档中AI生成部分的细粒度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM检测多聚焦于文档级分类，无法识别混合作者文档中具体哪些部分由LLM生成，缺乏细粒度的局部定位能力。

Method: 在Token层面操作，对现有Token级检测得分进行平滑处理以降低波动性，并利用自适应Lepski类型规则根据本地作者结构动态选择带宽；该方法无需Token级标注数据进行训练。

Result: 理论上证明了该方法在估计潜在信号时具有优良的平均平方误差性能；实验表明其在合成数据集和真实数据集上均优于多种基线方法，并已部署为公开可用的网站工具。

Conclusion: 该非监督的Token级平滑方法能有效解决LLM生成内容的细粒度定位问题，兼具理论保证与实证有效性。

Abstract: The rise of human-AI collaborative writing has created a growing need for fine-grained detection methods that support localizing likely LLM-generated content in mixed-authorship documents. Existing methods for detecting LLM-generated text mainly focus on document-level classification and cannot identify which parts of the text are generated by LLMs. This paper introduces a new method to address this urgent need. Our method operates at the token level, the natural unit of modern language models, and builds on existing token-level detection scores. The key idea is to smooth adjacent token scores to reduce their variability, while using an adaptive Lepski-type rule to select the bandwidth according to the local authorship structure. Our method is simple to implement and does not require token-level labeled data for training. Theoretically, we characterize this trade-off and show that the proposed method achieves favorable mean square error performance in estimating the underlying signal. Empirically, we demonstrate strong performance of our method against a wide range of baselines in both synthetic datasets and a realistic dataset. We deploy a publicly accessible website that implements the methods as well.

</details>


### [291] [AREX: Towards a Recursively Self-Improving Agent for Deep Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21461)
*Shuqi Lu,Chaofan Li,Kun Luo,Zhang Zhang,Hui Wang,Hongwang Xiao,Zheng Liu,Lei Xiong,Jiahao Wang,Sen Wang,Xiyan Jiang,Wanli Li,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Bingyu Yan,Ziyi Xia,Yingxia Shao,Kang Liu,Zhicheng Dou,Di He,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zhongyuan Wang,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AREX，一种递归自改进（RSI）深度研究智能体。利用发现-验证非对称性，通过内外循环交替进行证据收集与约束审计，并学习自主上下文更新工具压缩历史状态，在多个基准测试中显著优于同等规模基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究需要找到同时满足多个约束的答案，发现此类答案成本高昂，而验证候选答案可分解为可处理的逐约束检查。这种“发现-验证”的非对称性表明，智能体不应仅延长搜索时间，而应通过验证中间结果并利用部分验证状态指导后续细化来递归改进当前答案。

Method: 引入AREX框架，包含内层研究循环（收集证据、构建暂定答案）和外层自改进循环（按约束审计答案、识别未解决主张、发起针对性跟进研究）。为解决长周期RSI问题，训练自主上下文更新工具，将交互历史压缩为保留已验证证据和未解决约束的紧凑改进状态。通过代理式中期训练和长周期强化学习进行训练，并在关键步骤（获取决定性证据或纠正错误方向）强调奖励以缓解稀疏最终奖励问题。实例化为4B密集模型和122B-A10B混合专家模型。

Result: 在BrowseComp、WideSearch、DeepSearchQA、Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) 及其他推理和工具使用基准测试中，AREX显著优于同等规模的基线模型，并与激活参数多得多的模型保持竞争力。

Conclusion: AREX通过递归自改进机制和高效的上下文管理，有效解决了深度研究中多约束答案发现的难题，展现了强大的推理和工具使用能力，是提升AI深度研究性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Deep research requires agents to find answers that jointly satisfy multiple constraints. Discovering such answers is costly, whereas verifying a candidate can often be decomposed into tractable constraint-wise checks. This discovery--verification asymmetry suggests that a research agent should do more than simply search longer: it should recursively improve its current answer by verifying intermediate results and using the partially verified state to guide subsequent refinement. We introduce AREX, a family of Recursively Self-Improving (RSI) deep research agents. AREX alternates between an inner research loop that gathers evidence and constructs a provisional answer, and an outer self-improvement loop that audits the answer constraint-wise, identifies unresolved claims, and launches targeted follow-up research. To sustain RSI over long horizons, AREX learns an autonomous context-update tool that compresses growing interaction history into a compact improvement state preserving verified evidence and unresolved constraints, without relying on an external model. We train AREX on verified synthetic tasks and high-quality trajectories through agentic mid-training and long-horizon reinforcement learning. To mitigate sparse final rewards during long horizon learning, we emphasize key steps where decisive evidence is acquired or erroneous research directions are corrected. We instantiate a dense 4B model and a 122B-A10B Mixture-of-Experts model. Across BrowseComp, WideSearch, DeepSearchQA, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and other reasoning and tool-use benchmarks, AREX substantially outperforms comparable-scale baselines and remains competitive with models using substantially more activated parameters.

</details>


### [292] [Toward Continuous Assurance for the Democratization of AI Agent Creation in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21495)
*Natan Levy,Harel Berger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对非工程用户通过低代码/无代码环境创建的AI代理存在的可靠性问题，提出一种轻量级持续保障框架，结合依赖映射、就绪合同和定期检查来确保代理在部署后的长期稳定运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着非工程用户通过低代码、无代码及对话式开发环境创建AI代理，组织内的创新速度加快，但由此产生的“可靠性差距”日益凸显。这些代理看似简单的生产力工具，实则依赖于不断变化的模型、工具、检索源、权限、提示词、计划及外部服务。这种复杂性导致即使在没有用户直接修改的情况下，代理在部署后也可能出现长期的静默退化，影响其可靠性。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级的持续保障框架（continuous-assurance framework），专门用于公民创建的 organizational agents。该框架包含以下核心组件：1) 依赖映射（Dependency mapping）以识别关键依赖项；2) 就绪合同（Readiness contracts）定义操作标准；3) 定期检查（Scheduled checks）和诊断（Diagnostics）监控状态；4) 生命周期治理（Lifecycle governance）。此外，还开发了初始原型审计员，并通过基于场景的评估展示了如何将分类法转化为实际检查点和可操作的修复建议。

Result: 成功识别了民主化AI代理创建带来的可靠性挑战，并构建了一个综合性的持续保障框架。通过原型系统和场景评估验证了该框架的有效性，证明其能够将理论上的依赖关系分类转化为具体的实践检查手段和 actionable remediation guidance（可操作的补救指导）。

Conclusion:  democratized AI agent creation 虽然促进了快速本地创新，但也引入了显著的可靠性风险。通过实施包含依赖管理、合同约束和持续监控的轻量级保障框架，组织可以有效应对因外部依赖变化导致的代理性能退化问题，确保公民开发者创建的AI代理在生产环境中的长期稳定性和可操作性。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly created inside organizations by non-engineering users through low-code, no-code, and conversational development environments. This democratization enables rapid local innovation, but it also creates a reliability gap: agents that appear to users as simple productivity artifacts may depend on changing models, tools, retrieval sources, permissions, prompts, schedules, and external services. These dependencies can cause silent degradation long after deployment, even when no user directly modifies the agent. This paper identifies the reliability challenge created by democratized AI agent creation and proposes a lightweight continuous-assurance framework for citizen-created organizational agents. The framework combines dependency mapping, readiness contracts, scheduled checks, diagnostics, and lifecycle governance to assess whether an agent remains operationally ready under expected conditions. We also present an initial prototype auditor and scenario-based assessment showing how the proposed taxonomy can be translated into practical checks and actionable remediation guidance.

</details>


### [293] [Same Dangerous Objective, Opposite Advice: Direct Exposure versus Multi-Agent Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21518)
*Linjun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，高能力大语言模型（LLM）在面对经过“Id”和“Censor”转换及过滤的危险目标时，比直接暴露于原始危险指令时表现出更高的安全性与对齐度。这揭示了一种组合式安全漏洞：恶意目标可通过多阶段工作流绕过下游模型的审查，使其在不知晓原始操纵意图的情况下执行目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前高能力LLM在不同信息暴露程度下的安全性差异，特别是当危险目标经过中间代理转换和过滤后，模型的行为反应及其背后的安全机制漏洞。

Method: 使用OpenAI的gpt-5.6-sol模型别名进行测试，设定25种预定义的镜像权衡配置文件。实验对比了两种情境：一是模型直接暴露于授权隐藏、伪造和施压的危险目标；二是通过“Id”和“Censor”将同一目标转化为情感表达和约束重写后的意向，由仅看到偏好方向但未见原始操纵条款或来源的“Superego”组件接收并生成建议。

Result: 1. 行为反向转变：直接暴露于危险目标时，模型生成的建议反对其目标；而经过转换和过滤后，面向用户的“Superego”组件生成的建议与目标一致。
2. 组合式安全漏洞：高能力LLM可作为自动化多阶段工作流中的用户端组件，服务于明确具有操纵性的目标。上游保留原始指令和操纵条款，下游模型仅在无上下文感知情况下执行目标方向，且终端用户无法直接检查上游消息。

Conclusion: 当前高能力LLM可能识别或不信任操纵性动机，从而在间接暴露时表现出更高安全性。然而，这种机制导致了一个显著的安全缺口：恶意行为者可利用多阶段工作流结构，将危险目标分解并隐藏操纵意图，使下游模型在无意识中协助实现有害目标，而用户和系统难以察觉。

Abstract: Even a current high-capability LLM can appear safer when shown a dangerous objective directly than when other agents transform and relay its direction. Using OpenAI's gpt-5.6-sol model alias, we test 25 pre-specified mirrored trade-off profiles. Direct exposure to an objective authorizing concealment, fabrication, and pressure produced advice net opposed to its target. After an Id and Censor transformed the same objective into affect and a constraint-rewritten, target-bearing intention, the user-facing Superego---which saw the preferred direction but not the raw objective, its manipulative clauses, or its source---produced advice net aligned with the target.
  This behavioral reverse shift is consistent with the model recognizing or distrusting the manipulative motive, although we do not identify its internal mechanism. The second result exposes a compositional safety gap: a current high-capability model can be used as the user-facing component of an automated, multi-stage workflow serving an explicitly manipulative objective. The workflow can keep the raw instruction, its manipulation-authorizing clauses, and its provenance outside the downstream model's context while preserving the objective's target direction. A user with endpoint-only access likewise cannot directly inspect those upstream messages including the objective.

</details>


### [294] [MIRROR: Learning from the Other View for Multi-Modal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21552)
*Wen Ye,Yuxiao Qu,Aviral Kumar,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MIRROR方法，通过强化学习利用多模态几何问题中不同视图（文本、图像、图文结合）的互补推理路径，解决视觉语言模型在多模态推理中表现不一致的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型具备强推理能力，但视觉语言模型在视觉推理上存在不足，尤其是在处理同一问题的不同表示形式（纯文本、纯图像、图文结合）时表现出行为不一致性。现有的多模态后训练未能充分利用这些不同视图所暴露的互补推理路径和失败模式。

Method: 1. 构建ODA-Data数据集：包含高质量配对的几何问题数据，涵盖文本主导、图像主导及图文结合三种视图，并划分训练和评估集以研究模态依赖的推理行为。2. 开发MIRROR算法：一种基于强化学习的自监督优化方法。对于每个问题，MIRROR在所有视图下评估模型，选择表现最佳的视图作为“教师”，并使用反向KL散度目标函数训练其他视图向该教师学习。

Result: 在评估几何问题的推理基准测试中，MIRROR优于标准的强化学习方法，显著提高了模型在不同模态下的准确性和行为一致性。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模和利用多模态输入之间的差异与互补性，MIRROR能够有效提升视觉语言模型的跨模态推理能力和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Unlike large language models (LLMs) that exhibit strong reasoning capabilities, vision-language models (VLMs) struggle with visual reasoning, even on geometry problems that admit equivalent text, diagram, and combined diagram+text views. We show that these views often elicit different behaviors: a model may solve a problem from text but fail on the corresponding diagram, or succeed visually while failing textually. This inconsistency suggests that different views expose complementary reasoning paths and failure modes that standard multimodal post-training does not fully exploit. To study and exploit this phenomenon, we construct ODA-Data, a high-quality paired multimodal geometry dataset with text-dominant, image-dominant, and combined image+text views of the same problems, together with splits for training and evaluating modality-dependent reasoning behaviors. We then develop Modality-Informed Reciprocal Reasoning Optimization (MIRROR), a reinforcement learning approach for improving multimodal reasoning via self supervision. For each problem, MIRROR evaluates the model under all views, selects the best-performing view as a teacher, and trains other views with a reverse-KL objective towards the teacher. Across reasoning benchmarks that evaluate on geometry problems, MIRROR improves over standard RL and yields more accurate and consistent behavior across modalities

</details>


### [295] [Beyond Sycophancy: Structured Resistance and Compliance in LLM Moral Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21558)
*Baihui Wang,Bernard Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出LLM的社会校准不仅需减少阿谀奉承，还需区分何时采纳他人观点与坚持道德判断。通过三项研究，发现模型判断修正受视角距离、来源归属和联盟结构三个维度影响，将阿谀奉承重构为更广泛的社会影响过程，为区分建设性信念修正与顺从性合规提供基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够向他人学习而不盲目顺从的社会校准大型语言模型，需要超越仅将“阿谀奉承”视为单一失败模式的做法，解决模型在采纳他人观点与坚持有根据的道德判断之间的平衡问题。

Method: 通过三项研究，分析模型判断修正的结构，考察视角距离、来源归属和联盟结构对模型接受度和响应的影响，并将结果与人类社会心理学中的经典现象进行对比。

Result: 模型的判断修正沿三个维度结构化：1）新观点与初始立场的距离（模型更易接受接近的立场）；2）观点的来源归属（模型更易受被视为自身先前判断的观点影响）；3）支持该观点的联盟结构（模型对群体压力的响应不同）。这些发现表明阿谀奉宽泛地是社会影响塑造的判断更新过程的一种表现。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了区分建设性信念修正与阿谀奉承式顺从的原则性基础，有助于在道德关键性互动中实现更好的模型对齐。

Abstract: Building socially calibrated large language models, which can learn from others without simply yielding to them, requires more than reducing sycophancy as a one-dimensional failure mode. Models must distinguish when to incorporate others' perspectives from when to maintain a well-grounded moral judgment. We study the broader resistance-compliance process governing this distinction. Across three studies, we show that models' judgment revision is structured along three dimensions that parallel classic phenomena in human social psychology: the distance between an incoming view and the model's initial position, the source attribution of that view, and the coalition structure supporting it. Models are generally more receptive to nearby positions, more influenced by views presented as their own prior judgments, and differently responsive to group pressure. These findings recast sycophancy as one expression of a broader judgment-updating process shaped by social influence. Our framework provides a principled basis for distinguishing constructive belief revision from sycophantic compliance, thereby supporting better alignment in morally consequential interactions.

</details>


### [296] [Unsupervised Consensus-Based Anomaly Detection for Spatiotemporal Malaria Incidence in Ghana](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21559)
*T. Ansah-Narh,Y. Asare Afrane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究利用共识异常检测框架分析加纳2014-2023年疟疾监测数据，揭示了异常传播在时空上的结构性特征。研究发现，疟疾负担（病例数）与异常频率（行为持续性）存在空间差异：塔马利异常负担最高，而阿散蒂地区异常频率最高。异常月份具有显著更高的病例数和季节性偏差。结论指出，仅关注疟疾负担无法完整反映传播动态，区分高发区与异常行为区有助于优化 surveillance 和精准防控策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的疟疾监测往往侧重于疾病负担（即病例数量），但这可能无法全面反映传播动力学的复杂性。为了更准确地识别非典型传播模式并支持针对性的控制策略，需要一种能够区分“高负担”与“异常行为”的分析框架。

Method: 应用共识异常检测框架对加纳2014年至2023年的月度疟疾监测数据进行时空分析。通过计算异常负担（异常期间的累积病例）和异常频率（异常行为的持续性），结合统计学指标（如Cohen's d）比较异常月份与正常月份的病例数和季节性偏差。

Result: 1. 异常在时空上高度结构化，主要集中在阿散蒂地区和北部地区，持久热点包括塔马利、库马西和阿克拉。
2. 发现了异常负担与异常频率的空间区分：塔马利在异常期间拥有最高的病例负担，而阿散蒂地区的区县表现出最高的异常频率。这表明高负担区域不一定是异常传播最频繁的区域。
3. 异常月份形成统计上独特的群体，其病例数远高于正常月份（Cohen's d = 3.252），且季节性偏差较大（d > 1.2）。

Conclusion: 仅依靠疟疾负担无法提供关于传播动力学的完整图景。通过区分疟疾最流行的地区与传播行为最不寻常的地区，该框架能够加强监测体系，优先开展调查，并支持制定更具针对性的控制策略。

Abstract: A consensus anomaly detection framework was applied to monthly malaria surveillance data from Ghana (2014-2023) to identify atypical transmission patterns. Anomalies were highly structured in space and time. Ashanti and Northern Regions accounted for most recurrent anomalies, with persistent hotspots at Tamale, Kumasi, and Accra. A key finding was the spatial distinction between anomaly burden (cumulative cases during anomalous periods) and anomaly frequency (persistence of unusual behaviour). Tamale had the highest burden during anomalies, whereas the highest anomaly rates clustered in Ashanti districts, showing that high-burden areas are not necessarily those with the most frequent anomalous transmission. Anomalous months formed a statistically distinct group, with much higher case counts (Cohen's $d = 3.252$) and large seasonal deviations ($d > 1.2$) compared with normal months. Malaria burden alone provides an incomplete picture of transmission dynamics. By distinguishing where malaria is most prevalent from where transmission behaves most unusually, this framework can strengthen surveillance, prioritise investigations, and support targeted control strategies.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [297] [Demonstrating GenDB: Instance-Optimized and Customized Query Processing Code Generation via LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20630)
*Jiale Lao,Immanuel Trummer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GenDB 是一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的生成式查询引擎，通过将查询处理从手动工程转向代码生成，旨在解决传统数据库难以扩展和构建成本高的问题。它通过 LLM 代理为特定数据、负载和硬件生成实例优化的执行代码，并结合传统 DBMS 形成混合架构以平衡一次性查询与高频模板查询的性能需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统查询处理引擎因内部复杂而难以扩展，且支持新技术或新需求往往需要从头构建全新系统，这带来了巨大的工程 effort 和高昂成本。

Method: 提出 GenDB，利用 LLM 代理生成针对特定场景定制的查询执行代码。采用混合架构：对于离线重复性模板查询，通过前期代码生成并利用模糊测试和人工检查确保正确性；对于 ad-hoc 查询，由传统 DBMS 处理，GenDB 加速高频 SQL 模板。演示系统支持交互式探索工作负载分析、资源画像、计划生成及迭代优化过程。

Result: 在 TPC-H 和一个用于减少 LLM 训练数据泄露风险的新基准测试上，GenDB 生成的代码实现了显著优于最先进查询引擎的性能。用户可通过可视化界面定性分析其性能优势来源，并上传自有数据进行多 LLM 和查询模式的探索。

Conclusion: GenDB 证明了将查询处理转化为由 LLM 驱动的代码生成是可行的，并通过混合架构有效结合了传统数据库的通用性与生成式优化的特异性性能优势，为数据库系统的可扩展性和效率提供了新范式。

Abstract: Traditional query processing engines require continuous development and extensions to support new techniques and user requirements, and in some cases, entirely new systems must be built from scratch. However, these engines are difficult to extend due to their internal complexity, and building new systems demands significant engineering effort and cost. To address this, we demonstrate GenDB, a generative query engine that shifts query processing from manually engineered systems to query processing code generation driven by Large Language Models (LLMs). An early prototype of GenDB uses LLM agents to generate instance-optimized query execution code tailored to specific data, workloads, and hardware resources. This prototype suits offline code generation for repetitive, templated queries, since the upfront generation cost amortizes over many executions and correctness can be ensured through extensive fuzz testing and manual inspection. For ad-hoc queries, GenDB can work with a traditional DBMS in a hybrid architecture: the DBMS handles one-off queries, while GenDB speeds up frequent SQL templates. Our demonstration allows users to (1) visually and interactively explore how GenDB analyzes workloads, profiles hardware resources and underlying data, produces query plans, generates code based on them, and finally uses an optimizer to iteratively achieve a correct and efficient implementation; (2) use visual inspection and analysis to gain qualitative insights into why GenDB produces code that achieves significantly better performance than state-of-the-art query engines on two benchmarks: TPC-H and a newly constructed benchmark designed to reduce potential data leakage from LLM training data; and (3) upload their own data and queries to explore GenDB with different LLMs and query patterns.

</details>


### [298] [DMG: A Scalable and Efficient Memory-Disaggregated Graph Processing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20881)
*Yizou Chen,Tsun-Yu Yang,Zhisheng Hu,Baotong Lu,Ming-Chang Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出DMG，首个实用的基于解耦内存（DM）架构的图处理系统。通过DM友好型存储、自适应更新协调器和两阶段工作负载管理器，解决了现有方案扩展性差和缓存需求过大的问题，实现了计算与内存资源的弹性扩展及性能大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图处理系统基于单体服务器，导致资源利用率低；现有的DM架构图处理系统虽旨在解决此问题，但存在架构不可扩展（无法超越单节点限制）且对计算侧缓存需求过大（远超常规实践）等缺陷，导致其不切实际。

Method: 1. 提出DM友好型图存储结构并进行检索优化以提升效率；2. 设计自适应更新协调器，以低开销协调计算和内存节点执行更新传播；3. 采用两阶段工作负载管理器，结合粗粒度初始分区和细粒度运行时重调度以实现快速有效的负载均衡。

Result: 相比最先进的DM基图处理系统，DMG能弹性扩展计算和内存资源，性能提升最高达4.9倍，支持不断增长的图规模；同时将计算侧缓存需求降低高达18.9倍。

Conclusion: DMG具备优越的系统可扩展性和缓存效率，是一种具有实践意义的DM就绪解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional graph processing systems are built on monolithic servers, which couple a fixed ratio of compute and memory resources but often result in resource under-utilization in data centers. Although the disaggregated memory (DM) architecture has emerged to address this inefficiency, we identify that existing graph processing systems on DM remain highly impractical. They rely on unscalable architectures that fail to scale beyond a single memory node and a single compute node, and they require compute-side caches that are orders of magnitude larger than conventional practice in DM. To this end, this paper presents DMG, the first practical graph processing system on DM, which demonstrates superior system scalability and cache efficiency while delivering high performance. To improve efficiency of graph retrieval on DM, DMG proposes a DM-friendly graph store with retrieval optimizations. To mitigate costly update propagation, DMG presents an adaptive update coordinator that coordinates compute and memory nodes to perform update propagation with low overhead. To enable fast and effective load balancing, DMG employs a two-stage workload manager that includes a coarse-grained initial partitioning and a fine-grained runtime re-scheduling. Experimental results substantiate that compared with the state-of-the-art DM-based graph processing system, DMG can elastically scale up both compute and memory resources, delivering up to 4.9X better performance and accommodating graphs with ever-increasing sizes; meanwhile, it effectively tames the compute-side cache demands by up to 18.9X, positioning itself as a DM-ready solution in practice.

</details>


### [299] [A Logic-based Temporal Cohort Discovery Engine: Algorithms, Indices, and Experimental Results on the National Sleep Research Resource](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21377)
*Yan Huang,Xiaojin Li,Licong Cui,Guo-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出一种基于逻辑的睡眠研究队列发现引擎，利用理性集合逻辑（QEL）和生物医学事件结构时序模型（BEST），通过模型检查实现高效、形式化的时间序列查询，显著提升大规模生理数据检索速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型睡眠研究数据库中的队列发现多依赖非正式的脚本或简单的标量索引过滤器，缺乏形式化语义支持，难以处理复杂的时间关系和大规模数据的高效检索需求。

Method: 采用理性集合逻辑（QEL）作为稠密时间形式的查询基础，将标注的多导睡眠图表示为生物医学事件结构时序模型（BEST）。将队列发现问题转化为在BEST数据库上对QEL公式的模型检查问题。设计了三种可复用的时间查询模式：单事件检索、双事件时间模式匹配和事件数据提取。实现了基于Python的原型引擎，支持内存和MongoDB后端执行，并构建了线性空间和构建时间的2DFC索引结构。

Result: 在包含9000万个区间的数据集上，2DFC索引构建时间从RTFC的11,549秒和2DRT的23,902秒大幅降低至3,655秒。在真实世界克利夫兰儿童睡眠与健康研究（CCSHS）数据（515名受试者，202,587个区间）上，队列选择查询在原生规模下实现亚秒级延迟，在1000倍缩放规模下仍能在45秒内完成。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将形式化语义、模型检查和专用索引整合到生物医学信息学体系中，显著提升了大规模睡眠生理数据中复杂时间模式查询的效率与准确性，为符号生物医学提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Large sleep-study repositories contain rich time-stamped physiological annotations, but cohort discovery is still commonly implemented as ad hoc scripts or scalar-index filters. We present a logic-based temporal cohort discovery engine that brings formal semantics, model checking, specialized indexing, and empirical evaluation into a unified biomedical informatics framework. We adopt Rational Ensemble Logic (QEL) as a dense-time formal foundation for sleep-data querying and represent each annotated polysomnogram as a Biomedical Event Structure Temporal Model (BEST), a finite mapping from event labels to non-overlapping rational interval ensembles. Cohort discovery is formulated as model checking of QEL formulas over BEST databases. We organize common sleep-research requirements into three reusable temporal query patterns: single-event retrieval, dual-event temporal pattern matching, and event data extraction. The prototype cohort discovery engine was implemented in Python with in-memory and MongoDB-backed execution modes and evaluated on synthetic interval datasets containing up to 90 million intervals and on real-world National Sleep Research Resource annotations from the Cleveland Children's Sleep and Health Study (CCSHS) containing 515 subjects, 202,587 intervals, 23 event labels. 2DFC constructs indexes in linear space and linear build time, reducing build time at 90 million intervals from 11,549 s with RTFC and 23,902 seconds with 2DRT to 3,655 seconds. On CCSHS, cohort-selection queries executed at sub-second latency at native scale and under 45 seconds at 1,000 times scale. This work is a part of the Symbolic Biomedicine program championed by the corresponding author.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [Probabilistic Residual Learning for Online Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20863)
*Wenyuan Wang,Yusong Zhao,Zihao Xu,Hengyi Wang,Qi Xu,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Yi Wang,Zihao Zhao,Bo Long,Chengzhi Mao,Shuang Yang,Hengguan Huang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出概率残差学习（PRL），一种因果贝叶斯推荐模型，通过建模真实值与基础预测之间的残差，解决深度学习推荐系统黑盒性及计算复杂性问题，实现即插即用式性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于深度学习的推荐系统存在黑盒性质和计算复杂性，导致难以系统性增强其推荐能力。

Method: 提出PRL模型：(1) 概率性对用户进行分组以进行局部残差建模；(2) 建模影响用户和项目表示的领域级混杂因素；(3) 使用do-演算聚合特定簇的残差预测以消除混杂效应。

Result: 实验表明，PRL具有即插即用特性，兼容多种基础深度学习推荐系统，在提升性能的同时自动发现有意义的用户簇。

Conclusion: PRL通过因果推断方法有效增强了现有推荐系统的性能，并提供了可解释的用户聚类结果。

Abstract: Modern recommender systems are typically based on deep learning (DL) models, where a dense encoder learns representations of users and items. As a result, these systems often suffer from the black-box nature and computational complexity of the underlying models, making it difficult to systematically enhance their recommendation capabilities. To address this problem, we propose Probabilistic Residual Learning (PRL), a causal Bayesian recommendation model that models the residual between ground-truth and base predictions, enabling targeted refinement of existing systems. Specifically, PRL (1) probabilistically groups users for localized residual modeling, (2) models domain-level confounders that influence user and item representations, and (3) aggregates cluster-specific residual predictions over the confounders using do-calculus. Experiments demonstrate that our plug-and-play PRL is compatible with various base deep learning recommender systems, improving their performance while automatically discovering meaningful user clusters.

</details>


### [301] [LO-FAR: A Cost-Aware Local Filter for Sparse Feature Ranking in Industrial Ad Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20873)
*Egemen Erbayat,Luis Duque,Sohini Roychowdhury,Mohammad Amin,Srihari Reddy*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LO-FAR，一种仅CPU、模型无关的稀疏特征排序工作流，通过轻量级局部估计器而非GPU重训练来快速评估特征重要性，在工业级数据上实现了低成本且高效的特征筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业广告推荐模型依赖大量稀疏高基数ID列表特征，导致存储、训练和推理成本高昂。随着流量和模型演变，需要频繁进行特征选择，但传统基于置换或随机门控的方法计算成本高、迭代慢，因此急需一种受限于计算预算和时间周期的快速特征排序方案。

Method: 提出Localized Feature Ranking (LO-FAR)，这是一种仅使用CPU的工作流。它不依赖GPU绑定的重新训练循环，而是使用轻量级局部估计器，对每个候选特征在独立保留集上的预测信号进行单独排名，从而评估其特征重要性。

Result: 在包含超过100万条日志交互和475个稀疏ID列表特征的工业数据集上，LO-FAR仅需约2个CPU小时即可完成排序。在保留100-400个特征时，其在CTR和CVR任务上的Normalized Entropy增益与shuffle-based importance、Binary Stochastic Neurons及基于覆盖率的启发式方法相当。

Conclusion: 当面临严格的成本和周转时间约束时，简单的局部过滤方法（如LO-FAR）比更复杂的交互感知替代方案更具实用性和生产部署价值，为工业界提供了一种高效、低成本的特征工程解决方案。

Abstract: Industrial ad recommendation models rely heavily on sparse, high-cardinality ID-list features that encode user histories and contextual identifiers. Each is backed by a dedicated embedding table, so these features dominate storage, training, and serving cost and must be revisited as traffic and downstream models evolve. Therefore, sparse feature ranking is not just an offline modeling problem but also a recurrent systems decision limited by compute budgets and iteration cadence. We present Localized Feature Ranking (LO-FAR), a CPU-only, model-agnostic workflow that ranks each candidate feature from its stand-alone held-out predictive signal using lightweight local estimators rather than the GPU-bound retraining loops of permutation- and stochastic-gate-based methods. On a production dataset of more than one million logged interactions and 475 sparse ID-list features, LO-FAR completes ranking in approximately two CPU-hours and preserves downstream Normalized Entropy gains on CTR and CVR tasks that are competitive with shuffle-based importance, Binary Stochastic Neurons, and a coverage-based heuristic across budgets of 100--400 retained features. The contribution is a deployable workflow showing that, when cost and turnaround constraints are binding, a simple local filter can be a practical production choice over heavier interaction-aware alternatives.

</details>


### [302] [Controllable and Content-Based Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20938)
*Fırat Öncel,Jihoon Jeong,Emiliano Penaloza,Mirco Ravanelli,Laurent Charlin,Cem Subakan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CCBR框架，通过从物品内容（图像、音频、视频）直接推断文本摘要，将协同过滤模型与文本瓶颈结合，实现可解释、可控且支持多模态干预的推荐系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推荐系统依赖潜在密集表示，导致模型难以解释和控制；现有可控推荐系统在文本摘要生成上存在局限。

Method: 提出CCBR（Controllable and Content-Based Recommendations）框架，嵌入协同过滤模型，利用文本瓶颈实现可控性；直接从物品多模态内容（图像、音频、视频）推断文本摘要，而非依赖外部标签或用户反馈。

Result: 在图像、音频和视频数据集上，CCBR性能与标准潜在表示模型相当，且显著优于基线模型TEARS；通过系统性干预验证了用户可通过文本引导模型输出方向的有效性。

Conclusion: CCBR成功实现了基于内容的可控推荐，兼顾模型性能与可解释性，为用户提供了直观的干预手段，适用于多模态推荐场景。

Abstract: Traditional recommendation systems rely on latent (dense) representations, making them difficult to interpret and control. We propose the Controllable and Content-Based Recommendations (CCBR) framework, which builds its recommendations from textual user profile representations. CCBR plugs into collaborative filtering models and introduces controllability via text bottlenecks. We show that CCBR enables text-based and multimodal interventions, allowing users to steer the model towards the directions they prefer. Different from existing controllable recommendation systems, CCBR infers the text summaries directly from item contents (images, audio or video). Across image-, audio-, and video-based datasets, we demonstrate that the proposed framework obtains competitive model performance with standard (latent-representation) models while providing controllable model summaries via text. The model also outperforms TEARS, a recent baseline for controllable recommendation systems. Through systematic interventions, we demonstrate the efficacy of the user steering mechanism.

</details>


### [303] [Fast and Efficient Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search for High-Dimensional LLM Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20957)
*Nico Hezel,Kai Uwe Barthel,Bruno Schilling,Konstantin Schall,Andre Moelle,Klaus Jung*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了2026年SISAP索引挑战赛的提交方案，针对kNNG构建和MIPS任务，分别采用EVP量化结合重排序、以及维度增强结合FLAS预排序技术，以优化构建速度和查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在严格的约束条件下提升近似最近邻搜索（ANNS）算法的性能，具体解决高维嵌入下的kNNG构建效率和非归一化特征下的MIPS查询延迟问题。

Method: 1. kNNG构建：利用等 Voronoi 多面体（EVP）进行高效量化，并辅以针对性重排序策略以保持高召回率。
2. MIPS处理：通过维度增强将不对称内积问题转化为欧几里得搜索空间。
3. 优化机制：引入基于快速线性分配排序（FLAS）的一维预排序机制，在图构建前减少查询延迟并优化内存访问。

Result: 显著提升了后续图遍历过程中的空间局部性和缓存命中率，从而优化了整体性能。

Conclusion: 提出的方法有效解决了SISAP挑战赛中的关键任务，展示了在构建速度和查询效率方面的优化成果。

Abstract: The annual SISAP Indexing Challenge benchmarks Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (ANNS) algorithms under rigorous constraints. This paper presents our submissions for the 2026 edition, addressing both $k$-Nearest Neighbor Graph (kNNG) construction on 1024-dimensional BGE-M3 embeddings (Task 1) and Maximum Inner Product Search (MIPS) on unnormalized Llama-3.2-8B features (Task 2). To optimize construction speed, we utilize Equi-Voronoi Polytopes (EVP) for efficient quantization, supplemented by targeted reranking strategies to maintain high recall. For MIPS, we transform the asymmetric inner product problem into a Euclidean search space via dimensionality augmentation. To reduce query latency and optimize memory access, we introduce a 1D presorting mechanism via Fast Linear Assignment Sorting (FLAS) prior to graph construction. This significantly improves spatial locality and cache hit rates during subsequent graph traversal.
  Source Code: https://github.com/Visual-Computing/sisap26-deglib

</details>


### [304] [Bridging the Structural Gap: Adapting Autoregressive Generation for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21028)
*Junchao Zeng,Junzhang Zhu,Junyang Chen,Yudong Li,Wei Liu,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出BARGE模型，通过Item Context-Aware Attention恢复项目级结构，并利用Hierarchical Path Reranking和Dual-Path Decoding抑制语义漂移，解决生成式推荐中的结构与推理不一致问题。在公开基准和腾讯在线A/B测试中表现优异，显著提升点击率和阅读时长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于残差量化的生成式推荐（GR）存在两个结构性缺陷：1）将多token ID展平为单序列破坏了项目级结构；2）训练与推理间层级码本的不一致导致语义漂移。

Method: 提出BARGE模型：1）编码阶段采用Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA) 恢复项目级结构；2）解码阶段结合Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR) 和 Dual-Path Decoding (DPD) 从互补角度抑制语义漂移。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集及大规模离线测试中取得 superior 的推荐性能；腾讯平台在线A/B测试显示：点击率(CTR)提升0.60%，点击独立访客(CUV)提升1.34%，总阅读时长提升1.70%。

Conclusion: BARGE有效弥合了生成式推荐任务中的结构差距，兼具学术优越性与工业落地价值。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has emerged as a new paradigm for sequential recommendation, in which a representative line of work encodes items into hierarchical semantic IDs via residual quantization and predicts the IDs token by token. However, this generative formulation still exhibits structural gaps with respect to the recommendation task: flattening multi-token IDs into a single sequence destroys item-level structure, and the inconsistency between training and inference over a hierarchical codebook gives rise to semantic drift. To bridge these two gaps, we propose BARGE, which employs Item Context-Aware Attention (ICA) to restore item-level structure during encoding, and Hierarchical Path Reranking (HPR) together with Dual-Path Decoding (DPD) to suppress semantic drift from two complementary angles during decoding. Extensive experiments and analytical studies on public benchmarks and a large-scale offline test demonstrate that BARGE achieves superior recommendation performance. An online A/B test on a Tencent platform yields improvements of 0.60% in click-through rate, 1.34% in click unique visitors, and 1.70% in total reading time, confirming the practical value of BARGE in industrial-scale recommendation.

</details>


### [305] [SHIFT: Self-reconstruction Harnesses Implicit Fine-grained Thinking for Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21333)
*Yuxiao Luo,Da Li,Mingjie Zhang,Zhentao He,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SHIFT框架，通过自重建机制解决LLM检索器中隐式推理与生成目标不匹配的问题，利用残差投影和双向注意力聚合提升效率，在推理密集型检索基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的检索器（如GIRCSE、LaSER）虽通过隐式推理提升了效率，但未能有效解决检索目标与生成目标之间的不匹配问题，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出SHIFT（Self-reconstruction Harnesses Implicit Fine-grained Thinking for Retrieval）框架：1. 在潜在空间中使用残差投影和任务导向的双向注意力聚合，将LLM转化为推理高效的检索器；2. 使用基于细粒度下一个token预测的重建技术，缓解对比学习与隐式推理之间的不匹配。

Result: 在多个推理密集型检索基准测试中，SHIFT consistently（一致地）优于其他广泛使用的检索器，并通过详细分析验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: SHIFT框架通过结合高效的结构化转换和细粒度的自重建机制，成功弥合了检索与生成目标间的差距，为LLM驱动的检索系统提供了更优的训练范式。

Abstract: LLM-based retrievers have become a fundamental component of modern information retrieval systems. The paradigm of "rewrite-then-retriev" introduces explicit reasoning before retrieval. In addition, implicit-reasoning retrievers such as GIRCSE and LaSER improve efficiency by replacing explicit reasoning with soft tokens. Although these methods demonstrated competitive performance on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks, they struggle to address the mismatch between the objectives of retrieval and generation. In this work, we propose SHIFT ($\textbf{S}$elf-reconstruction $\textbf{H}$arnesses $\textbf{I}$mplicit $\textbf{F}$ine-grained $\textbf{T}$hinking for Retrieval), a retrieval training framework based on LLMs. Firstly, we transfer LLMs into reasoning-efficient retrievers with residual projection and task-oriented bidirectional attention aggregation in the latent space. Secondly, we alleviate the mismatch between contrastive learning and implicit reasoning using fine-grained next-token-prediction-based reconstruction. Extensive experiments on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks show that SHIFT consistently outperforms other widely used retrievers. We also carried out a detailed analysis to illustrate how our method works.

</details>


### [306] [Diffusion Language Model for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21519)
*Chengyi Liu,Yongqi Zhou,Junwei Pan,Zhixiang Feng,Chengguo Yin,Haijie Gu,Jie Jiang,Yinghao Liu,Yujuan Ding,Qing Li,Wenqi Fan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DLMRec，一种基于离散扩散语言模型的推荐系统，以克服传统自回归方法在捕捉物品结构依赖和纠错能力上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推荐的自回归范式存在缺陷：下一词预测侧重序列顺序而非用户偏好的结构化依赖；前缀约束生成限制双向上下文，导致早期错误累积且无法修正。

Method: 提出DLMRec（离散扩散语言模型推荐），包含三个核心组件：1）协作感知随机分词器，将多跳协作信号编码为离散token；2）课程驱动训练策略，通过渐进式项目/令牌级学习对齐去噪过程与偏好恢复；3）稳定性感知投票机制，聚合迭代预测以提升一致性和鲁棒性。

Result: 提供了一种替代自回归生成的有效方案，通过扩散建模更好地捕捉复杂用户偏好，并解决自回归解码中的错误累积问题。

Conclusion: 离散扩散语言模型为推荐系统提供了比自回归范式更具优势的新途径，能有效处理用户偏好的结构性依赖并提高生成质量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-empowered recommender systems have emerged as a promising paradigm for generative recommendation, leveraging their strong semantic reasoning and generative capacity to model complex, diverse user preferences. However, most existing approaches rely on an autoregressive paradigm that is suboptimal for recommendation. The next-token objective emphasizes sequential order rather than the structural inter-item dependencies underlying user preferences. In addition, prefix-constrained generation restricts bidirectional context and commits to left-to-right decoding, causing early errors to accumulate without correction. Inspired by the success of diffusion language models, we propose \textbf{DLMRec}, a discrete diffusion language model tailored for recommendation that offers a compelling alternative to autoregressive generation. Specifically, DLMRec introduces three key components to bridge diffusion language modeling with recommendation. First, a collaborative-aware stochastic tokenizer encodes multi-hop collaborative signals into expressive discrete tokens compatible with diffusion modeling. Second, a curriculum-driven training strategy aligns the denoising process with preference recovery through progressive item- and token-level learning. Third, a stability-aware voting mechanism aggregates iterative predictions to improve generation consistency and robustness.

</details>
